### [1] [ParseBench-2: Severity-Aware Evaluation of Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17400)
*Pierre-Loïc Doulcet,Boyang Zhang,Sebastián G. Acosta,Sacha Bron,Daniel B. Ospina,Antonio Jimeno Yepes,Nutchapol Dendumrongsup,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过严重性政策开发解析度量的方法，该政策定义了每种错误类型如何影响评分。通过编码代理优化度量实现，同时结合详细注释和有针对性的困难案例进行评估，确保度量结果更贴近专家判断，且对检测到的误差进行最少3.4%的惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文档解析度量可能高估对重要事实的更改并低估保持意义的更改。为了使度量设计更贴近人类优先事项，本文提出了一种基于严重性政策的方法，通过编码代理对比较规则、错误检测器和权重进行优化，同时进行详细注释和有针对性的困难案例评估。

Method: 本文通过定义严重性政策，利用编码代理优化解析度量实现，评估包括详细注释和针对困难案例的测试集，确保度量结果更贴近专家判断，并且少惩罚3.4%的非恶意变化。

Result: 本文在基准测试ParseBench-2上进行了评估，覆盖4,317页，六个解析质量维度。新的度量方法的评估结果更能与专家判断匹配，对检测到的错误最多只惩罚3.4%的无害变化。

Conclusion: 文章表明，通过严重性政策来优化解析度量可以更好地反映人类的优先事项和专业知识。

Abstract: Document-parsing metrics can give high scores to outputs that change important facts and penalize changes that preserve meaning. People can often judge these consequences more easily than they can design a metric to measure them. We introduce a method for developing parsing metrics from severity policies that specify how each error type should affect the score. We express these policies through test scenarios that specify how a metric should behave. We then optimize the metric implementations against these fixed expectations, using coding agents to refine comparison rules, error detectors and weights under human review. This makes stated human priorities an explicit objective of metric design. We apply this method in ParseBench-2, a comprehensive document-parsing benchmark that combines broad capability coverage with metrics calibrated to error severity. It covers 4,317 pages across six dimensions of parsing quality: text, formatting, tables, charts, visual grounding, and forms. We use detailed annotations and targeted difficult cases to check for changed facts, broken structural relationships, and missing content. We take the lower of a parsing score that measures content recovery and a severity cap that penalizes detected errors, so recovering most of a page cannot hide a serious error. Across six severity test suites, our metrics more closely match the expert judgments expressed by the scenario targets than existing metrics and penalize at most 3.4% of the tested benign variations. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation spanning frontier vision-language models, specialized document parsers and open-weight systems, comparing their overall rankings, performance across parsing capabilities and tradeoffs between accuracy and cost.

</details>


### [2] [Real-Time Diffusion Relighting as a Neural Sensor: Closing the Low-Light Domain Gap for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17438)
*Shengjie Xu,Alexander Gao,Anshul Rai,Dae Yeol Lee,Guan-Ming Su,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种集成生成神经传感器的实时感知架构，用于解决低光照条件下的机器人感知问题。该架构在控制环中运行大型图像到图像扩散模型，并仅在检测置信度下降时激活，从而显著减少计算开销。该方法在ExDark低光照基准测试中达到87.1%的mAP，并在自主驾驶模拟器中展示了将高延迟生成模型与标准机器人控制自主栈集成的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照条件严重影响了机器人的感知能力，而现有的低光照图像增强方法因效率问题难以实时应用。因此，研究提出了一种新的方法，将生成神经传感器嵌入控制环中，以实现实时感知。

Method: 该研究方法包括提出一种实时感知架构，该架构通过在控制环中运行大型图像到图像扩散模型来处理低光照问题，并根据检测置信度的下降激活系统，从而降低计算开销。

Result: 该方法在ExDark低光照基准测试中达到87.1%的mAP，并在自主驾驶模拟器中实现了100%的环导航完成率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地实现了在低光照和恶劣天气条件下机器人感知性能的提升，并提供了在闭环自主驾驶模拟器中集成高延迟生成模型的实验证据。

Abstract: Low-light conditions severely degrade robotic perception: object detectors trained on daylight imagery are prone to failure under dark and adverse weather conditions that fall outside of their training distribution. Furthermore, from an efficiency standpoint, existing Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) methods are designed for offline processing and are therefore impractical to integrate into live control loops. To address these issues, we propose to embed a Generative Neural Sensor within the control loop that treats illumination as a generative prior rather than a signal processing problem, enabling task-relevant semantic recovery. We further propose an Real-Time Perceptual Architecture that decouples high-latency semantic recovery from low-latency robotic control. Rather than blocking the control loop, our system runs a large-scale image-to-image diffusion model in a background thread, and is activated only when a drop in detection confidence signals a perceptual uncertainty event, significantly reducing computational overhead. Our method sets a new state-of-the-art performance on the ExDark low-light benchmark with 87.1% mAP, and under adverse weather conditions, significantly boosts detection rates over competing methods. On a navigation task in a closed-loop autonomous driving simulator (CARLA), we achieve a 100% lap completion rate, establishing a simulation-based proof-of-concept for integrating high-latency generative models with standard robotic control autonomy stacks.

</details>


### [3] [Adversarial Tracking: Attacks on Image Sensors for Object Tracking in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17505)
*Han Wu,Alex Chan,Ekin Can Erkus,Tousif Rahman,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了YOLOv3和YOLOv4检测器在图像特定和图像无感知攻击下的鲁棒性，结果表明在图像特定攻击下，基于YOLOv3的跟踪器性能显著下降，而基于YOLOv4的跟踪器更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于基于神经网络的检测器对对抗性攻击较为敏感，而其对下游轨迹估计的影响尚未完全由检测精度来表征，因此本文旨在评估图像特定攻击和图像无感知攻击对不同检测器和跟踪系统的影响。

Method: 使用CARLA模拟器对YOLOv3和YOLOv4检测器配以不同跟踪系统（分别为SORT、Deep SORT、Strong SORT和OC-SORT）进行评估，通过使用对抗性攻击方法（包括图像特定和图像无感知攻击），测量跟踪质量时采用MOTA、HOTA和IDF1等指标。

Result: 在图像特定攻击条件下，基于YOLOv3的跟踪器HOTA下降到14.85-17.03%，MOTA在最坏情况下达到-772.60%，而基于YOLOv4的跟踪器HOTA保持在25.06-26.86%；在图像无感知攻击条件下，使用单一通用对抗性扰动能够显著降低跟踪准确性。

Conclusion: 基于YOLOv4的跟踪系统比基于YOLOv3的更具抗攻击性，而图像无感知攻击对跟踪准确性的影响更为严重。

Abstract: Object tracking systems estimate the motion of surrounding vehicles by linking detected objects across video frames. In particular, Tracking-By-Detection (TBD) systems combine object detectors with motion prediction and data association to maintain object identities over time. However, neural network-based detectors are vulnerable to adversarial attacks that perturb input images, and their impact on downstream trajectory estimation is not fully characterized by detection accuracy alone. Therefore, this paper evaluates image-specific and image-agnostic attacks on YOLOv3 and YOLOv4 detectors paired with SORT, Deep SORT, Strong SORT, and OC-SORT tracking systems. The evaluation uses CARLA simulator and measures tracking quality through Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA), Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA), and Identification F1 (IDF1). Under image-specific attacks, HOTA for YOLOv3-based trackers declines from 53.45-56.19% to 14.85-17.03%, while MOTA reaches -772.60% in the worst case. YOLOv4-based trackers exhibit greater robustness, while HOTA still declines to 25.06-26.86%. In addition, image-agnostic attacks can reduce the tracking accuracy using a single Universal Adversarial Perturbation (UAP), without generating a separate perturbation for individual frames.

</details>


### [4] [Rethinking Visual Reasoning in Text-to-Image Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17585)
*Shiye Su,Xiaohan Wang,Ludwig Schmidt,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现广泛使用的奖励模型在视觉推理评估中表现较差，提出了通过视觉推理监督改进偏好学习的方法，显著提升了视觉理解和偏好预测，改进了文本转图像生成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高现代文本转图像系统的性能，特别是解决广泛使用的奖励模型在视觉推理上的不足，作者提出了新的方法改进奖励模型。

Method: 作者首先发现现有的奖励模型在视觉推理上的缺陷，包括从强大的视觉语言骨干网络进行微调和持续学习基线的不足。然后，通过将视觉推理监督引入偏好学习来改进奖励模型，具体做法是将视觉推理数据集转换为偏好数据，并在混合数据集上进行训练。

Result: 通过这一方法，奖励模型在视觉理解和偏好预测上均有提升，并且在复杂的组成提示下，文本转图像生成任务的性能相比于HPSv3提高了4.0% 和5.5%。

Conclusion: 这一创新方法展示了通过视觉推理数据优化奖励模型的有效性，未来可以在其他视觉理解任务中进一步应用这种新的整合方法。

Abstract: Reward models are a core component of modern text-to-image systems, serving as the objective for inference-time selection and RL-based post-training. A key prerequisite is visual understanding: to judge prompt adherence and ground aesthetic preferences, a reward model must bind attributes, resolve relations, and count reliably. We find that widely-used reward models perform poorly on targeted visual reasoning evaluations. Even when starting from strong vision-language backbones, preference fine-tuning erodes these capabilities, and continual-learning baselines fail to mitigate a steep tradeoff between visual reasoning and preference accuracy. We propose a data-centric intervention that injects visual reasoning supervision into preference learning by flipping the pairing axis. To complement conventional human preference data (paired images under a shared prompt), we convert existing visual reasoning datasets into preference pairs that share the same image but differ in prompt, and train on the mixture. This yields a Pareto improvement in both visual understanding and preference prediction. Across inference-time best-of-n selection and RL post-training, the resulting reward model improves text-to-image generation on challenging compositional prompts (GenEval) by an average of 4.0% and 5.5% respectively, outperforming HPSv3 which was trained on 6x more preference pairs.

</details>


### [5] [Where to Predict in Retinal OCT? Anatomy-Guided Target Selection for I-JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17586)
*Yunchu Feng,Wai Tak Lau,Kaveri A. Thakoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了基于图像强度和预测的眼底 OCT 分割进行解剖指导的目标选择，以提高无监督学习中自编码预测架构（I-JEPA）的效果。结果表明，基于图像强度的矩形目标选择在比较中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习旨在从未标记的数据中学习有意义的表示，可能允许模型捕捉患者的细微解剖学变化，并减少所需的标记数据量。I-JEPA通过预测可见上下文中的目标区域表示，而不是重建像素。然而，它的均匀目标放置不考虑眼底OCT中的解剖结构。

Method: 研究提出了基于图像强度和预测的解剖学指导的目标选择方法，同时保留了I-JEPA架构和潜在目标预测目标。通过线性探测对FairVision上的青光眼分类效果进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于图像强度的矩形目标选择在对比中表现最佳。虽然更具体的解剖学选择有潜力，但并不是一直都能提升性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在眼底OCT的无监督表示学习中具有潜力，但更具体的解剖学选择不一定一直能提高性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning aims to learn meaningful representations from unlabeled data, potentially allowing models to capture subtle anatomical variations across patients and reduce the amount of labeled data needed. Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) learns by predicting target-region representations from visible context rather than reconstructing pixels. However, its uniform target placement does not account for the anatomical structure of retinal OCT, where retinal tissue occupies only part of each B-scan. We investigate anatomy-guided target selection using image intensity and predicted retinal segmentation while retaining the I-JEPA architecture and latent prediction objective. We compare anatomy-guided rectangular targets, anatomy-shaped targets and coverage-constrained placement with uniform masking, and evaluate the learned representations using linear probing for glaucoma classification on FairVision. We found that rectangular targets guided by image intensity achieved the highest linear-probe AUC in our comparisons. We show that anatomically meaningful target selection is promising for retinal OCT representation learning, while greater anatomical specificity did not consistently improve performance.

</details>


### [6] [Training-Free Acceleration of Multi-View 3D Transformers via Regime-Aware Attention Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17629)
*Muhammed Izzet Sezer,Sevval Belkis Dikkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究分析了三种多视图Transformer在七个数据集上的全局注意力机制，发现每一层都有特定的角色。在此基础上，提出了RAWATTA（Regime-Aware Attention Approximation），一种不需要训练的方法，在8位精度下运行注意力，仅在需要时在特定层保持16位精度，并且还提出了RAWATTA-Fast版本以进一步提高速度和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现现有3D重建方法的成本随着图像数量的增加而呈二次增长，因此提出了一个新的注意力机制来减少加速成本。

Method: 研究团队首先对三种多视图Transformer在七个数据集上的全局注意力机制进行了分析，根据分析结果，提出了不需要训练的方法RAWATTA 和 RAWATTA-Fast。

Result: 实验结果显示，与已发布的代码相比，RAWATTA和RAWATTA-Fast在保持相当重建精度的情况下显著提高了VGGT的运行速度，尤其是在处理大量图像时。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过合理分配不同层的精度，可以显著提高多视图3D重建的效率和精确度。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D transformers such as VGGT reconstruct a scene from a set of unposed images in a single forward pass, predicting camera poses, depth and 3D points, but the cost of their global attention grows quadratically with the num- ber of images. Accelerators borrowed from large language models assume that each token attends to only a few oth- ers; on these models they lose accuracy or gain little speed. We study the global attention of three multi-view transform- ers on seven datasets and find that every layer plays one of three roles: it averages over the whole scene, matches points across many images, or anchors on the first image, whose coordinates the outputs use. Building on this anal- ysis, we propose Regime-AWare ATTention Approximation (RAWATTA), a training-free method that runs attention in 8- bit precision and keeps 16-bit precision only in the layers where a worst-case calibration check requires it. Its fast set- ting, RAWATTA-Fast, also approximates attention in a few averaging and anchoring layers, while matching layers and the first image keep all their keys. On a 12 GB GPU at 50 im- ages, RAWATTA runs VGGT faster than the released code, mostly through our CUDA engine, and loses at most about one point of pose accuracy (AUC@3) on any dataset; RAWATTA-Fast is slightly faster and less accurate. Both fit 200 images, where the released code runs out of memory at 100.

</details>


### [7] [Provable Cross-Modal Transfer of Hierarchical Concept Structure in CLIP Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17671)
*Nghia Nguyen,Tianjiao Ding,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了CLIP等对比视觉-语言模型的嵌入为何展现了与语义概念层次结构一致的层次结构，提出了一种称为HAS的几何属性，并通过理论分析和实验验证了这种现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，CLIP等对比视觉-语言模型能够将图像和文本嵌入相匹配，但尚未探讨这种层次结构是如何从非层次化的训练目标中自然生成的。

Method: 本文首先定义并形式化了层次化角度分离（HAS）的几何属性，接着通过理论分析不完全受限特性模型，发现图像层级结构对文本嵌入的影响，最后通过合成实验验证理论。

Result: 研究揭示了图像文本对的分层次共现结构如何引导文本嵌入向根中心的图像原型偏斜，为理解CLIP模型的层次结构提供了一种机制，同时通过理论推导确认了具有线性对比损失时这一机制的显性表达，并将分析扩展到InfoNCE损失。

Conclusion: 总之，研究从理论上解释了为什么CLIP模型的文本嵌入在经过对比学习后能够体现语义概念的层次结构，并表明HAS属性不仅限于分析的范围，还能在更广泛的条件下被观察到，这证明了层次化角度分离现象的普遍性。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP are trained to align images and text, not to represent taxonomies. Yet empirical studies show that CLIP embeddings exhibit hierarchical structure aligned with the hierarchy of semantic concepts. A theoretical characterization of why such hierarchy emerges from a non-hierarchical objective remains open. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as Hierarchical Angular Separation (HAS), a geometric property whereby each non-leaf concept embedding is more similar to its descendants than to off-branch concepts. Empirically, we show that HAS holds approximately in raw images and randomly initialized CLIP image embeddings, while in text embeddings it holds approximately only after contrastive learning. To theoretically explain this asymmetry, we analyze an unconstrained features model in which the image embeddings are fixed and only the text embeddings are trained. At the global minimizer, the text-side HAS violation percentage at each node is at most the image-side HAS violation and near-violation percentage. The key mechanism is the hierarchical co-occurrence structure of image-text pairs, which biases text embeddings toward root-centered image prototypes. For a linear contrastive loss, we derive a closed-form solution that makes this mechanism explicit. We extend the analysis to the InfoNCE loss and establish analogous guarantees under sufficient regularization. Synthetic experiments validate the theory and show that HAS is transferred even beyond the regimes covered by our analysis.

</details>


### [8] [Open-World Scene Deocclusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17695)
*K. M. S. J. Kumarasinghe,Neil A. Dodgson,Fang-Lue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需训练的开放式场景去遮挡框架，能够从单张RGB图像中恢复出被遮挡物体的完整外观，这些物体可以是检测到的或用户选择的，框架将场景分解为几何合理、语义忠实且与可见场景视觉一致的深度排序RGBA层，实验表明其在真实场景中的去遮挡效果更好，重建结果更连贯和现实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的单一图象中不完全目标恢复方法在背景表面或未显式检测的遮挡物体，以及自然场景中常见的循环遮挡依赖性方面存在局限性，因此需要开发一种全新的去遮挡框架。

Method: 该框架通过将场景分解为深度顺序的RGBA层，以几何上合理、语义上一致且与可见场景视觉一致的方式恢复非完全可见物体的完整外观，实现无需训练的开放式场景去遮挡。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在真实场景中的去遮挡表现更好，能够产生连贯且现实感强的重建。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个新的无训练开放式场景去遮挡框架，突破了当前方法的局限，为场景理解和图像编辑提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recovering the complete appearance of occluded objects from a single image is a fundamental problem for scene understanding and image editing. Real-world scenes are challenging due to clutter, complex spatial occlusions, and diverse object categories. Existing methods often struggle when occlusion involves background surfaces or objects that are not explicitly detected, and fail to resolve the cyclic occlusion dependencies that frequently occur in natural scenes. We introduce a training-free, open-world scene deocclusion framework that recovers the full amodal appearance of detected or user-selected objects from a single RGB image, decomposing the scene into depth-ordered RGBA layers that are geometrically plausible, semantically faithful, and visually consistent with the visible scene. Extensive experiments show that our approach improves scene-level deocclusion, producing coherent and realistic reconstructions in real-world settings. Code will be available on the project page: https://sashjk.github.io/owsd/

</details>


### [9] [RaFL: An Empirical Study of Random Filter Losses for Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17707)
*Ruibo Wang,Fengbei Liu,Aleksei Tiulpin,Mert R. Sabuncu,Heejong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种随机滤波损失（RaFL），不依赖预训练特征或特定任务的损失工程，通过随机卷积、随机傅里叶特征和随机小波滤波器自动平衡多种滤波器的贡献。通过在多项任务上的验证PSNR和随机种子下的一致性，证明了RaFL在2D和3D图像重建任务中的有效性和稳定性，且计算成本较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的像素损失可能无法捕捉到目标分布中的细纹理，而感知损失则面临预训练特征领域特定的挑战，因此提出了一种名为随机滤波损失（RaFL）的新方法，旨在提供一种简单且无需手动调整的损失目标。

Method: RaFL使用随机卷积、随机傅里叶特征和随机小波滤波器，通过梯度范数匹配自动平衡这些滤波器的贡献。探讨了滤波器族、权重重新采样周期和权重初始化三种设计选择，并最终选择了结合所有三种滤波器家族、每优化步骤重新绘制滤波器和采用高斯初始化的配置。

Result: 在三个2D和两个3D图像重建任务上，RaFL在三项任务中表现优于像素损失，在两项任务中表现与像素损失相同。此外，RaFL证明了在所有任务上随机种子下的稳定表现。与使用VGG19预训练特征的感知损失相比，RaFL的计算成本更低，所需计算量仅为VGG19感知损失的1/182。

Conclusion: 随机滤波损失（RaFL）提供了一种简单、无需调整、适用于2D和3D图像重建任务的损失目标，不仅提高了模型的泛化能力，还大幅降低了计算复杂度，是一项有潜力的新方法。

Abstract: Pixel losses train image reconstruction networks toward a point statistic of the target distribution, often averaging away fine textures under uncertainty. Perceptual losses alleviate this issue using pretrained features, but those features are inherently domain-specific. We study a random filter loss (RaFL) that compares prediction and target through filters with random weights, avoiding both pretrained features and task-specific loss engineering. RaFL combines random convolutions, random Fourier features, and random wavelet filters, automatically balancing their contributions through gradient-norm matching, eliminating manual loss-weight tuning and grid search. We systematically investigate three design choices: filter family, weight resampling schedule, and weight initialization. Based on validation PSNR and stability across random seeds, we select the configuration that uses all three filter families, redraws filters every optimization step, and employs Gaussian initialization. We evaluate RaFL on three 2D and two 3D image reconstruction tasks. RaFL outperforms a pixel loss on three tasks and matches it on two, and demonstrates stable performance across random seeds on all five tasks. RaFL adds less than 2% to the backbone's forward FLOPs and no inference cost, requiring $182\times$ fewer loss FLOPs than a VGG19 perceptual loss. These findings suggest that RaFL is a simple, tuning-free, and domain-agnostic loss objective for 2D and 3D image reconstruction.

</details>


### [10] [QCAP-VQA: Question-Conditioned Capped Prior-Mass Supervision for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17708)
*Yining Zhao,Catarina Moreira,Mukesh Prasad,Ali Braytee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCAP-VQA模型通过问题条件化的先验引导部分交叉注意力头，确保了对标注结构的关注度，同时避免了过度依赖。该模型在无需空间监督的情况下达到了与基线相同的准确率，并且随着正则化强度增加，模型的表现与专家参考有所偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自动区域先验可能存在噪声，且其影响难以量化。QCAP-VQA模型旨在通过问题条件先验引导交叉注意力，提供更准确和可靠的结果。

Method: QCAP-VQA通过在部分交叉注意力头上应用问题条件化先验（从排名的分割提议中构建），并使用一个上限先验质量目标函数来监督模型注意力。该目标函数只在先验质量低于阈值时惩罚注意力不足。

Result: QCAP-VQA模型在没有空间监督的情况下达到了与未监督基线相同的准确率（84.26% vs. 84.23%），并通过调整正则化强度保持了合理的注意力分布范围（0.67到0.77），而传统方法会将其推至0.97并导致准确率下降。

Conclusion: 该研究强调，临床应用中的医学视觉问答系统应依赖于独立的解剖学参考，以评估自动先验的监督效果。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering models can answer correctly without attending to the anatomy that the question names. Automatic region priors allow spatial supervision without expert masks, but they are noisy, and it is unclear how strongly they should shape attention and how their effect should be verified. We present QCAP-VQA, which supervises a subset of cross-attention heads with a question-conditioned prior built from ranked segmentation proposals. Its capped prior-mass objective penalizes insufficient attention inside the prior only below a threshold. We evaluate attention against SLAKE structure boxes that play no role in training, separating attention on the named anatomy from how attention shifts between questions about the same image. QCAP-VQA matches the accuracy of a baseline without spatial supervision (84.26% vs. 84.23%), and every evaluated prior-supervised model places more attention on the annotated structures. Over a 64-fold range of regularization strength, the capped objective keeps prior mass between 0.67 and 0.77, whereas a pooled region-mass objective drives it to 0.97 and loses accuracy. The cost lies in question conditioning: as the constraint widens or strengthens, attention shifts agree less with the expert-defined contrast. Across 13 configurations, prior-referenced and expert-referenced evaluation rank the models in largely opposite order (Spearman correlation of -0.87), and the capped models it favors track the prior's own errors. Because clinical use of medical visual question answering requires answers grounded in the queried anatomy, supervision from automatic priors should be judged against independent anatomical references.

</details>


### [11] [HarmonyText: Harmonious Scene Text Editing via Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis and Region-Decoupled Frequency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17733)
*Ziqi Yi,Huiguo He,Guitao Xu,Peirong Zhang,Cheng Da,Xinyao Yu,Zhanpeng Wang,Changqian Yu,Huan Yang,Kun Gai,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HarmonyText的无掩码场景文字编辑框架，旨在通过基于区域和频率的监督优化，解决由伪GT监督、过度平滑文本生成以及缺乏GT无监督评价带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前场景文字编辑方法存在的问题包括伪标注监督不可靠、过度平滑的文本生成以及缺乏真实标注情况下的评估方法，导致高频率细节的抑制和文字边界模糊。

Method: HarmonyText框架引入了Region-Decoupling Optimization (RDO)，通过优化编辑区域与伪目标的对齐并与源图像保持一致性，实现了对场景文字的针对性修改。同时，使用Cascaded High-Frequency Texture Guidance (CHFTG) 和Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis (RePair)等技术，确保了边界锐利和细节纹理的保留，构建了高质量的配对数据集STE25K。

Result: HarmonyText在文字编辑准确性、背景一致性、颜色一致性以及感知和谐度方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HarmonyText为无掩码场景文字编辑提供了新的解决方案，通过有效的监督和增强技术，显著提高了编辑质量，同时提出了无标注重评协议。

Abstract: Natural language-guided, mask-free scene text editing aims to modify text in real-world images using only textual instructions, yet remains challenged by unreliable pseudo-GT supervision, over-smoothed text generation, and the absence of GT-free evaluation. Existing methods often rely on imperfect pseudo-targets with background discrepancies, while standard MSE-based velocity regression tends to suppress high-frequency details, leading to blurred text boundaries and weakened local textures. To address these issues, we propose HarmonyText, a mask-free scene text editing framework with region- and frequency-aware supervision. HarmonyText introduces Region-Decoupling Optimization (RDO), which aligns edited text regions with pseudo-targets while anchoring preserved regions to the source image, and Cascaded High-Frequency Texture Guidance (CHFTG), which applies cascaded wavelet-domain constraints to preserve sharp boundaries and fine-grained textures of textual content. We further propose Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis (RePair) to construct STE25K, a high-quality 25K paired dataset, and introduce a GT-free evaluation protocol with Color Distribution Divergence and a Harmony Assessment Model. Extensive experiments show that HarmonyText outperforms existing methods in text editing accuracy, background fidelity, color consistency, and perceptual harmony.

</details>


### [12] [Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17752)
*Yan Xing,Zhi Chen,Fanglue Zhang,Junhong Zhao,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过引入线裔家族dropout (SLD) 和线裔平衡稠密化 (LBD) 方法，本文提出了一个在稀疏视图3D重建中有效处理过拟合问题的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于可用的图像数量有限，3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 易于过拟合。传统的 dropout 方法对这个问题不太有效，因此需要新的方法来改进 dropout 以更好地处理过拟合。

Method: 本文提出了 Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout (SLD) 和 Lineage-Balanced Densification (LBD) 方法。SLD 在结构正则化阶段应用线裔家族 dropout，而在后期精炼阶段则切换为个体 dropout。LBD 在稠密化阶段构建扩展候选池，并根据不同家族的平均梯度分数和已有后代大小重新分配稠密化预算。

Result: 在 LLFF、MipNeRF360 和 Blender 数据集上的实验表明，所提出的框架在稀疏视图重建方面表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入新的 dropout 和稠密化方法，显著改善了 3DGS 在稀疏视图重建中的效果，克服了传统方法的不足。

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is prone to overfitting because only a limited number of images are available to constrain scene geometry. Dropout is one regularization strategy for alleviating this problem. We introduce the concept of a lineage family to describe a set of Gaussian primitives derived from the same founder through cloning or splitting operations. The founder Gaussians refer to all Gaussian primitives generated during initialization. Descendants within the same family often jointly contribute to the refinement of adjacent local regions. However, dropping individual Gaussians can be less effective due to the compensation effect from neighboring Gaussians for the dropped ones. To address this issue, we propose Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout(SLD), which applies lineage-family dropout during the structural regularization stage and switches to individual dropout during the later refinement stage. We further propose Lineage-Balanced Densification(LBD), which operates during densification by constructing an expanded candidate pool and reallocating the densification budget according to the mean gradient scores and existing descendant sizes of different families. Experiments on the LLFF, MipNeRF360, and Blender datasets show that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance in sparse-view reconstruction

</details>


### [13] [VACO: Vertex-Aligned Decoding for Dense Hand Contact Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17755)
*Binhong Ye,Jin-Chuan Shi,Tao Liu,Xin Wang,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VACO，一种顶点对齐的解码器，通过预测每个MANO顶点在其投影位置的局部图像特征，提高了密集手接触估计的准确性，打破了五个基准的记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接预测模型通过共享查询解码整个顶点接触图，导致顶点在图像中未接地且接触覆盖相邻非接触表面，影响接触估计精度。因此提出了VACO来改进这一点。

Method: VACO使用顶点对齐的解码器，其中每个MANO顶点在投影位置采样局部图像特征。同时预测手重建和接触的部分骨干网络在推理时一起预测这些位置，使用噪声扰动的地面真实投影和端到端特征适应来提高鲁棒性和接触敏感特征。

Result: VACO在一个单个检查点上在五个涵盖手物体、手人类、手场景和双手工操作的基准上建立了新的最先进水平。在Hi4D上F1分值提高了20.7%，并在HOT3D和HOCap的测试集上分别取得了5.5%和2.7%的增益。此外，接触预测还改善了后续的HOI重建。

Conclusion: VACO通过顶点对齐的解码策略显著提高了手接触估计的精度，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Dense hand contact estimation from a single RGB image is a foundational primitive for dexterous manipulation and 3D interaction analysis. Direct predictors typically decode the entire per-vertex contact map from one shared query, leaving individual vertices ungrounded in the image and smearing contact across adjacent, non-touching surfaces. We present VACO, a vertex-aligned decoder in which each MANO vertex samples local image features at its projected location. A hand-reconstruction backbone, jointly trained for reconstruction and contact, predicts these locations at inference without an auxiliary reconstructor. Training uses noise-perturbed ground-truth projections and end-to-end feature adaptation to improve robustness and contact-sensitive features. A single VACO checkpoint sets a new state of the art on five benchmarks spanning hand-object, hand-human, hand-scene, and bimanual interactions. It improves F1 by +20.7 pp over the strongest prior direct predictor on Hi4D and generalizes to the held-out HOT3D and HOCap, with gains of +5.5 and +2.7 pp. The resulting contact predictions also improve downstream HOI reconstruction.

</details>


### [14] [Efficient Genotype Classification from 3D MRI in ADPKD via Density-Aware Point Sampling and E(3)-invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17758)
*Kwanghee Lee,Hyeongsub Lee,Youngwoo Kim,Hyunji Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为E3PN的高效3D MRI基于ADPKD基因型分类网络，通过点采样和E(3)-不变性，在保持一定精度的同时大幅提高了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于ADPKD的某些基因型会导致快速的肾脏增长和早发性终末期肾病，因此早期检测非常重要。然而，传统的遗传测试成本高且数据量不足，难以训练深度学习模型。因此，研究人员希望开发一种基于MRI的低成本 genotype 检测方法。

Method: 研究采用了点表示的3D MRI，对比传统体素表示，节省了大量的GPU内存。此外，E3PN通过坐标规范化引入E(3)-不变性来进一步提高数据效率。

Result: E3PN在ADPKD数据集上训练速度提升了163倍，使用了103倍少的GPU内存，并在F1评分上从0.165提高到0.226，其中0.226为两种基线的最佳结果。这些结果为5分类的ADPKD基因型分类设定了首个基准，并证明了点采样和坐标规范化带来的极大的效率提升。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，点采样的3D MRI和坐标规范化的E(3)-不变性不仅大幅提高了模型效率，还在一定程度上提高了分类性能，为ADPKD的早期基因型分类提供了一个有竞争力的方法。

Abstract: Early detection of genetic kidney diseases, especially autosomal dominant polycystic kidney disease (ADPKD), is crucial because certain genotypes such as truncating PKD1 mutations cause rapid kidney growth and early progression to end-stage kidney disease. MRI-based genotype inference is a potential low-cost complement to genetic testing. However, although many deep learning methods have been developed for classifying various MR images, applying them to ADPKD remains challenging: (1) abdominal MR images are generally too large to be handled by deep learning models designed for smaller images like brain MRI, and (2) the amount of labeled data for ADPKD genetic mutations is insufficient to train deep learning models. In order to address these challenges, we propose E3PN, an efficient 3D MRI-based ADPKD genotype classification network that relies on point sampling and E(3)-invariance. We utilize a point representation of 3D MRI, which consumes less GPU memory than a voxel representation. For further memory efficiency, 3D points are sampled from a mixture distribution over the two kidneys in each image. Since E(3)-invariance has been shown to improve data efficiency, we also incorporate it into our model through coordinate canonicalization. On our ADPKD dataset, E3PN trains 163 times faster and uses 103x less GPU memory than a 3D ResNet, while also improving F1 from 0.165 to 0.226 over both baselines. These results establish a first benchmark for five-way ADPKD genotype classification from 3D MRI and show that point sampling and coordinate canonicalization yield two-orders-of-magnitude efficiency gains with a modest but consistent improvement in F1.

</details>


### [15] [Creative Product Design under Brand Image Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17762)
*Takeshi Onishi,Hiroya Makino,Takahiro Yamaguchi,Hiroyuki Sakai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种品牌感知再设计生成框架，通过品牌形象调查报告和自增强循环生成符合品牌约束的创意设计，实验表明该框架在汽车和摩托车等高度受限产品类别中表现尤为出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像的方法在生成创意设计时对品牌形象这类特定且隐含的约束考虑不足，因此需要一种能够增强品牌感知的方法来指导设计生成。

Method: 该研究构建了一个品牌感知再设计生成框架，通过品牌形象调查报告和自增强循环生成设计。首先，基于产品名称生成品牌形象调查报告，提取报告中的创意方向；然后，将此方向融入自增强循环中，生成的图像会被批判评估，从而反复调整生成策略。

Result: 在五个产品类别上进行的50项再设计任务中，该框架在品牌识别能力和创意性之间的表现优于现有方法，特别是在汽车和摩托车等高度受限的类别中表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了动态提取和文本化品牌形象可以指导符合品牌形象约束的创意产品设计生成。

Abstract: Industrial design requires creative visual exploration while satisfying constraints that are often product-specific and implicit. Existing text-to-image (T2I) approaches can generate creative designs through concept blending or multi-agent self-enhancement, but they provide limited mechanisms for ensuring that generated designs remain consistent with such constraints. We focus on brand image as a representative design constraint and propose a framework for brand-aware redesign generation. Given a product name, the framework constructs a brand image survey report that summarizes product and market information, derives a creative direction from the report, and integrates this direction into a self-enhancement loop in which generated images are critiqued and prompts are revised. We evaluate the framework on 50 redesign tasks across five product categories, comparing it with Naive T2I, IP-Adapter/IT-Blender, and unconstrained self-enhancement under the same T2I backbone. By a VLM-as-Judge protocol to measure creativity and brand identifiability, the proposed framework achieves a more favorable trade-off between the two objectives, with particularly clear gains in highly constrained categories such as automotive and motorcycle, where the plausible design space is relatively limited compared with that of other product categories. These results suggest that dynamically eliciting and textualizing brand image can guide creative product design generation under brand image constraints.

</details>


### [16] [Learning Anisotropic Prediction Targets for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17767)
*Rayhan Zirvi,Hansheng Chen,Gordon Wetzstein,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnisoFlow是一种新的生成模型，它通过自适应噪声放大来优化目标预测，并在图像生成任务中显著提高了图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于流的生成模型依赖于预先确定的预测目标，从而影响了训练质量和样本质量。AnisoFlow通过学习可变的噪声组件尺度，以更灵活的方式来优化目标预测，这解决了传统方法的局限性。

Method: AnisoFlow在保持原有结构和损失函数不变的情况下，通过学习一组非线性权重来放大目标预测中的噪声成分，从而优化了预测。它通过联合学习基矢来实现自学习目标预测，并通过精确映射从网络预测中恢复速度。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256数据集上，AnisoFlow使用相同的架构和损失函数，仅优化预测目标，就将FID得分从1.90降至1.55。在像素空间的文本图像生成中，AnisoFlow提高了图像质量，减少了低精度推理下的贴图接缝伪影。

Conclusion: AnisoFlow展示了通过灵活优化目标预测来提高生成模型性能的巨大潜力，特别是在图像生成任务中，其可自学习的目标预测机制在提高图像质量方面更加有效。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are trained on a hand-chosen prediction target, such as the velocity, the noise, or the clean data. This choice strongly affects training and sample quality. We introduce Anisotropic Flow Models (AnisoFlow), which replaces a fixed prediction target with a fully learnable one. AnisoFlow learns to scale the noise component of the target anisotropically, assigning a separate nonnegative weight to each direction in a jointly learned basis. A closed-form map recovers the velocity from the network's prediction, leaving the architecture, flow-matching loss, and sampler unchanged. Since recovery is exact, training optimizes the target purely to make it easier for the network to predict. We theoretically analyze the resulting prediction error and predict that the preferred noise weight in each direction scales near-linearly with data variance, in agreement with the weights learned empirically in pixel space. AnisoFlow improves FID from 1.90 to 1.55 over the JiT baseline on ImageNet 256$\times$256, by learning only the prediction target, without representation alignment or architectural changes. In pixel-space text-to-image generation, AnisoFlow improves image quality and, through better-conditioned velocity recovery, largely removes patch-seam artifacts under reduced-precision inference.

</details>


### [17] [Towards Seconds-level Video Representation: Quadratically Gated Feature Grid with Adaptive, Prediction-free Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17786)
*Tie-Yun Li,Hao-Ran Wang,Chao Wang,Zhiyong Yin,Yiming Xu,Zhenwei Cai,Li Li,Rongzhi Lin,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Q-GAS 是一种新型的视频表示方法，通过结合多分辨率显式 3D 特征网格和低秩局部二次门解码器，以及误差感知、不依赖预测采样器，提高了表达能力和效率，能够在 GPU 上实时解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的隐式神经视频表示方法因生成速度缓慢而难以在工业应用中普及，Q-GAS 提出了一种新的方法以克服这一瓶颈。

Method: Q-GAS 采用多分辨率显式 3D 特征网格，结合低秩局部二次门解码器和误差感知、不依赖预测的采样器。

Result: Q-GAS 能在 RTX PRO 6000 单卡上以小于 50 秒的时间完成拟合和量化，解码速度超过 370 FPS（混合精度）和大约 146 FPS（单精度）。相较于现有的 Instant-NGP 基线，Q-GAS 的训练和量化速度快 5.5-7.2 倍，相较于 RNeRV 速度快 19-28 倍。在 90 秒预算内，Q-GAS 在 PSNR、MS-SSIM 和 LPIPS 方面优于评估的 NeRV 家族流水线。

Conclusion: Q-GAS 方法提供了一种高效且实时的视频表示，显著提高了现有的性能指标。

Abstract: Implicit neural video representations are notoriously slow to build, which does not cross the basic practical threshold for most industrial applications. Here, we present Q-GAS, a coordinate-queryable video representation that couples multi-resolution explicit 3D feature grids with a compact SwiGLU-style quadratic-gating (QG) decoder and an error-aware, prediction-free sampler. The explicit grids avoid hash-collision coefficient sharing at the cost of modestly higher storage yet greatly boost the expressive capacity; the QG decoder introduces low-rank local second-order interactions to improve the nonlinear feature response. During fitting, a global error map periodically refreshes a high-error pool, and each batch mixes pool samples with uniform samples, which is free from the auxiliary model to predict difficult samples, thereby further escalating the efficiency. On UVG Beauty/Bosphorus/Jockey, Q-GAS fits and quantizes under 50 s on one RTX PRO 6000, decodes at over 370 FPS with mixed precision and about 146 FPS in FP32. In our PyTorch implementation, Q-GAS trains and quantizes 5.5-7.2 times faster than the evaluated Instant-NGP baseline and 19-28 times faster than RNeRV. Under a 90 s budget, it improves PSNR/MS-SSIM/LPIPS over the evaluated NeRV-family pipelines at comparable BPP over the measured overlap. We have shared our code at https://github.com/MillenRosen/Q-GAS for readers' free use.

</details>


### [18] [Can Large Language Models Optimize Pretrained Neural Network Weights? A Multi-Phase Empirical Evaluation of Gradient-Free Editing in YOLO26 Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17789)
*Mohamed Abdallah Salem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过评估5个实验阶段的YOLO26模型，使用MS COCO数据集，比较了不同的直接权重编辑方法。结果表明，增加CMA-ES评估次数、混合LLM-CMA-ES方法和真实的LLM建议没有明显的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索直接权重编辑方法作为预训练探测器微调的无梯度替代方案，同时验证大型语言模型（LLM）的指导效果。

Method: 使用YOLO26模型和MS COCO数据集，采用了确定性缩放、随机扰动、差分进化、CMA-ES、结构化干预、真实本地LLM提议、失败意识指导和混合LLM-CMA-ES等方法进行实验，并通过原始Ultralytics mAP50-95对结果进行优化。

Result: 增加CMA-ES预算至250次评估，开发收益从+0.11个百分点提高到+0.28个百分点；混合LLM-CMA-ES方法没有在任何预算下显示统计上显著优于随机策略指导的优势（p值分别为0.612、0.369和0.694）；对YOLO26m的五次种子复制分析仅显示出微小的COCO API AP差异；225个存档的LLM提案中，只有53个符合最终严格方案。

Conclusion: 真实LLM干预的权重编辑在开发集外显示潜在价值有限；尽管实际LLM介导的权重编辑是可行的，但当前测试的策略并没有展示出持续的成功转移或明显的LLM优势。

Abstract: Direct weight editing offers a gradient-free alternative to fine-tuning pretrained detectors, but its generalization and the value of large language model (LLM) guidance remain uncertain. We evaluate five experimental phases using YOLO26 models and MS COCO, comparing deterministic scaling, random perturbations, differential evolution, CMA-ES, structured interventions, real local-LLM proposals, failure-aware guidance, and hybrid LLM-CMA-ES against a matched random-policy control. Optimization uses native Ultralytics mAP50-95 on 1,000 COCO Val2017 images; selected candidates are assessed on a disjoint, previously examined 1,500-image subset, with COCO API AP50-95 reported separately where available. Increasing the CMA-ES budget from 8 to 250 evaluations raised development gains from +0.11 to +0.28 percentage points, while selection changes remained negative (-0.03 to -0.12 points); the 250-evaluation result uses one seed. The hybrid LLM approach showed no statistically established advantage over random-policy guidance (p = 0.612, 0.369, and 0.694 at budgets 8, 25, and 50). Five-seed YOLO26m replication yielded a COCO API AP difference of +0.004 points (95% CI: -0.026 to +0.034). Of 225 archived LLM proposals, 53 satisfy the final strict schema; early tensor-level execution cannot be fully verified. Genuine LLM-mediated weight editing is feasible, but the tested approaches did not demonstrate consistent transfer beyond the development subset or a measurable LLM advantage.

</details>


### [19] [SceneAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Portable Game Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17821)
*Xingyi Li,Jiahao Yang,Yiqin Zhu,Jingwen Ye,Yang Sun,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneAgent 是一个多代理框架，旨在实现跨游戏引擎的场景构建过程和经验的重用，通过标准化场景上下文和后端适配器分离持久场景状态、任务本地推理和可重用经验，提高场景构建的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前场景生成器移植到新游戏引擎需要更多工作，包括计划、构造工具和存储经验，依赖于原始后端。SceneAgent 旨在解决这一问题。

Method: SceneAgent 采用多代理框架，利用标准化场景上下文和后端适配器分离持久场景状态，通过结构化信息手工业务查询，并在不同代际中提炼人类批准的操作技能和语义内容技能。同时，引入了 GameSceneBench 评测基准。

Result: 在 GameSceneBench 评测基准下，SceneAgent 在五个基线中有 78.2-89.4% 的人类偏好调整分数，并实现了技能和多代理协议的可重用性，提高了指令对齐和场景完整性。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，可以将持久场景状态、任务本地推理和可重用经验分离出来，以实现跨引擎的场景构建过程和经验的重用，提高了灵活性和效率。

Abstract: Moving a scene generator to a new game engine requires more than exporting its final output: planning, construction tools, and stored experience may also depend on the original backend. We present SceneAgent, a multi-agent framework for reusing the construction process and its experience across backends. A canonical SceneContext maintains the evolving world, while backend adapters isolate coordinate conventions, asset bindings, and export. Structured handoffs let fresh Builder sessions query this persistent state without inheriting the full construction transcript. Across generations, human-approved traces are distilled into asset-ID-free Layout skills and semantic Content skills that can be retrieved and recombined. We introduce GameSceneBench, comprising 69 indoor and outdoor prompts. Under a common asset and evaluation protocol, SceneAgent obtains human tie-adjusted preference scores of 78.2-89.4% against five baselines. Ablations show that reusable skills and the multi-agent protocol primarily improve prompt alignment and scene completeness, with additional runtime. Deployment in a second engine preserves the shared construction protocol and skill library while replacing backend integration components. Together, these results support separating persistent scene state, task-local reasoning, and reusable experience for portable scene construction.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Endpoints: Bézier Paths for Vision Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17825)
*Chenzhi Zheng,Xi Xiao,Chih-Ting Liao,Qizhen Lan,Jingwen Lin,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过学习共享的提示令牌，可以将冻结的视觉基础模型适应新的任务或数据变化。该研究探讨了提示在连续变化过程中能否沿着最短路径移动。文章提出了共享注意力的额外运输成本，量化了提示沿最短路径移动的成本差异，并展示了使用二次贝塞尔曲线路径可以减少特征行动，从而提高在未标记数据分布转移情况下的准确性和在线准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的研究试图探讨提示在视觉基础模型适应新任务或数据变化时的极限以及移动方式。研究关注于共享提示与图像内容之间的权衡，以便更好地理解提示沿着最短路径移动的可能性及其限制。

Method: 研究使用了精确可解的注意力模型来分析可到达的特征形成锥体如何展开为可解决问题。此外，该研究探讨了不同数量的提示、小的图像内容扰动和对齐多头输出情况下的限制。研究还比较了使用二次贝塞尔曲线路径与线性和交叉熵训练路径在保持中等准确性的基础上，其在未标记数据分布转换下的表现。

Result: 分析揭示了一种新的内容归一化门槛，当端点具有相同的符号时，最多种类数量为两，才能使共享提示沿最短路径移动。即使有更多提示、小的图像内容扰动以及对齐的多头输出，这一差距依然存在。在ViT-B/16和VTAB-1k的数据集上，使用二次贝塞尔曲线路径可以减少未标记数据分布转移情况下的特征行动，从而提升准确性和在线准确性。此外，对比研究还发现，即使端点不连续，使用二次贝塞尔曲线路径所形成的提示路径也可能能够绕过最短路径。线性和交叉熵训练的路径虽然在具有校准标签的条件下保持更高的最小准确率，但在未标记数据分布转移时的表现较弱。

Conclusion: 研究发现提示能否沿最短距离移动不仅依赖于终点之间的距离，还与提示本身如何移动相关。因此，为了找到更优的提示路径，应当更加关注不同终点之间的动态变化关系以及如何使图像的自身内容和共享提示之间的权衡趋于一致。

Abstract: Frozen vision foundation models can be adapted by learning a few prompt tokens shared across all images. When prompts are updated as data shift or tasks change, the distribution of image features moves from one endpoint to another. Prior theory asks which endpoints prompts can reach; we ask whether they can travel between them along the shortest path. Because shared prompts compete with each image's own content for attention, they often cannot. We quantify this restriction as the extra transport cost that shared attention imposes over the shortest path. In an exactly solvable attention model, the reachable features form a cone; unrolling it into a plane gives the exact minimum action for every pair of endpoints and explicit optimal prompts on short arcs. The analysis reveals a sharp threshold in content normalization, which measures how much attention each image's own tokens absorb: for same-sign endpoints, at most two distinct levels allow prompts to follow the shortest path, whereas three or more force a strictly positive extra cost unless the endpoints differ only by rescaling. This gap persists with more prompts, small content perturbations, and aligned multihead outputs. On ViT-B/16 with VTAB-1k, a quadratic Bézier prompt path that minimizes feature action reduces held-out transport action relative to linear and cross-entropy-trained paths between the same endpoints and improves intermediate and online accuracy under unlabeled distribution shift, while cross-entropy-trained paths retain higher minimum accuracy when labeled calibration data are available. Reachable endpoints do not guarantee a reachable shortest path: how prompts move matters, not only where they end. Project page: https://xixiaouab.github.io/Beyond-Endpoints/.

</details>


### [21] [Signal Scaffolding: Unlocking the Potential of Lightweight Models for Self-Supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17828)
*Zhijie Wang,Li Zhang,Yujie Chen,Shibiao Deng,Xin Gao,Houzhi Zhou,Jiangshuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的轻量级单目深度估计训练策略 Signal Scaffolding (SS)，通过在不增加额外数据集或参数的情况下应用该策略，显著提高了轻量化网络ReForgeDepth的性能，达到了在准确性和效率之间取得前沿表现，并能够在边缘设备上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级单目深度估计方法通常性能较差，因为传统训练策略未能充分挖掘轻量化模型的潜力。本文旨在通过提出一种新的多阶段训练策略 Signal Scaffolding (SS)，探索如何更好地利用轻量化模型的潜在能力。

Method: SS策略分为三个阶段，旨在逐步优化模型性能。该方法应用在由作者设计的轻量化网络ReForgeDepth上进行实验。

Result: 在KITTI和Cityscapes数据集上的实验表明，采用SS策略的方法在准确性和效率之间取得了领先的表现，并且能够在边缘设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: 本文的研究表明，轻量化模型的潜力远未完全开发，而关键可能在于训练策略而非参数分配。

Abstract: With the rising demand for edge devices, lightweight self-supervised monocular depth estimation methods have continued to evolve; however, existing methods generally suffer from poor accuracy. We find that the root cause may lies in the failure of conventional training regimes to fully exploit the inherent potential of lightweight models. To address this, we propose a novel three-phase training strategy for lightweight self-supervised monocular depth estimation, termed Signal Scaffolding (SS). Without introducing any additional dataset or parameters, we apply SS to ReForgeDepth, a lightweight network of our own design. Experiments on the KITTI and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off and running in real time on edge devices. These results suggest that lightweight models can be improved further within their existing capacity, and that the central question may be less about how parameters are allocated than about how to unlock this latent potential through training.

</details>


### [22] [V-JEPA-LM: Video-Language Models That Read Dynamics Beyond Static, Text-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17831)
*Keuntae Kim,Dongjun Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入基于事实的监督，增强了世界模型视频编码器V-JEPA的语义理解和应用能力，在多个视频基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型视频编码器虽然可以学习场景变化，但在回答问题时缺乏对视频内容的整体理解，导致性能受限。

Method: 研究者通过转向基于像素变换的公开视频数据，并使用单阶段训练方法，将视频帧作为输入，输出匹配问题的答案，并在此过程中增强了语言模型的读取能力。

Result: 使用V-JEPA和语言模型结合的方法（V-JEPA-LM），在多个视频基准测试中表现优异，尤其是在速度问题上的表现更加显著。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过合理设计监督机制，可以显著提升基于视频的大型语言模型的表现，并在实际应用中取得了显著成果。

Abstract: World-model video encoders such as V-JEPA learn how scenes change (direction, speed, order, state) from video alone. To answer questions, they are joined to a language model trained on captions and question-answer pairs. But each caption or question names only a few attributes of a clip, so the model learns to retrieve those attributes rather than to read the clip, and the dynamics the encoder carries go unused: on the same items, a linear probe reads speed from frozen V-JEPA features at 87% while the VLM answers at chance. We show where dynamics lives in a frozen encoder: in a subspace shared across videos for predictive encoders such as V-JEPA, but not as a shared component of frame-wise, text-aligned encoders such as SigLIP. We then build counterfactual supervision that makes the language model read it: pixel transformations turn public videos into answer-balanced variants whose answers are set by the transformation and differ only in the change. Trained in a single stage on 4.53M public samples with 32 visual tokens per frame, where open video LLMs use several stages, 9M to 172M samples and 64 to 256 tokens per frame on average, the resulting V-JEPA-LM models (1B to 8B, Qwen3 and Llama) lead every size class in average score across eleven video benchmarks; our 4B model scores 72.1, against 61.1 for the best open 4B and 64.8 for the best open 8B model. A controlled encoder x supervision study shows that the encoder decides what can be read and the supervision what is read: at a matched budget, counterfactual supervision adds 4.5 points on average and 38 on the speed questions of natural benchmarks. Code and models: https://github.com/kkt94/Llama-V-JEPA-LM.

</details>


### [23] [PADSNet: Physics-Aware Dual-Stream Network with Biomechanical Priors for Laryngeal Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17852)
*Zhenzhen You,Weifeng Yu,Zhenghao Shi,Shuangli Du,Minghua Zhao,Yingcheng Zhao,Xinhong Hei,Demin Kong,Xiaoyong Ren,Jin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过提出PADES网络，该研究旨在通过结合视觉表征和显式的生物力学推理来精确分类声带病变，从而在临床数据集上实现高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型主要依赖于视觉外观，忽视了具有临床意义的声带振动动态信息，文章旨在解决该问题。

Method: 研究设计PADES网络，该网络包含：1) 多尺度小波注意力增强模块，用于弥合光谱语义差距，共同学习空间纹理和动态频率表现；2) 知识计算模块，从时间声门距离波形中提取可解释的生物力学索引，涵盖声带活动性、对称性、声门闭合和振动稳定性；3) 物理解算控制模块，将生物力学先验整合到视觉特征中，自适应地重新调整相关于病理的振动模式。

Result: 在临床数据集上实现84.52%的准确率，F1分数为80.90%，灵敏度为79.53%，特异性为88.66%。

Conclusion: 明确地将生物力学先验与深度视觉表示结合，可以为声带病变的自动化分类提供更加准确、鲁棒且临床可解释的框架。

Abstract: Precise classification of laryngeal lesions is clinically crucial yet highly challenging, as inter-class visual differences are often subtle. While abnormalities in vocal fold vibratory dynamics provide complementary discriminative information, existing deep learning models predominantly rely on visual appearance and lack explicit mechanisms for modeling clinically meaningful vibratory dynamics. To address this, we propose PADSNet, a Physics-Aware Dual-Stream Network for laryngeal lesion classification that integrates visual representations with explicit biomechanical reasoning. Specifically, a Wavelet-Attention Enhancement Module (WAEM) is designed to bridge the spectral-semantic gap, enabling complementary learning of spatial texture and dynamic frequency representations. Meanwhile, a Knowledge Computing Module (KCM) extracts interpretable biomechanical indices from temporal glottal distance waveforms, including indicators of mobility, symmetry, glottal closure, and vibration stability. These descriptors are subsequently incorporated into a Physics-Aware Gating Module (PAGM) as biomechanical priors to adaptively recalibrate visual feature toward pathology-relevant vibratory patterns. Extensive experiments on a clinical dataset demonstrate that PADSNet achieves an accuracy of 0.8452, an F1-score of 0.8090, a sensitivity of 0.7953, and a specificity of 0.8866. These results indicate that explicitly integrating biomechanical priors with deep visual representations provides a more accurate, robust, and clinically interpretable framework for automated laryngeal lesion classification.

</details>


### [24] [DoubleTake: Focused Verification of Visual Evidence for Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17853)
*Kejun Xue,Ang Li,Jinghua Tang,Xincheng Wang,Yongbin Gao,Yanghua Xiao,Zhijun Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DoubleTake 是一种无需训练的框架，通过两次阅读与验证视觉证据的过程，提高长文档问答系统对视觉证据的处理能力。与直接使用原始问题进行验证相比，使用聚焦事实问题进行验证可以显著提高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于视觉语言模型在长文档问答中可能忽视相关证据，从而导致错误答案，DoubleTake 提出了一种无需训练的框架来专注于视觉证据的验证。

Method: DoubleTake 包含两次阅读和验证过程：第一次阅读产生候选答案和证据记录；第二次阅读中，通过比较两次候选答案与证据，形成一个具体事实问题；然后验证者从源图像中回答该事实问题，最后推理器基于验证者的回答解决原始问题。

Result: DoubleTake 通过冻结 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 模型，实现了四个评估基准中的最高整体准确性。在使用相同页面和模型调用次数时，提出聚焦事实问题比再次提出原始问题的验证更为准确。

Conclusion: DoubleTake 框架在提高视觉证据验证准确性方面具有显著优势，尤其是在长文档问答场景下。该方法的代码可在指定的 GitHub 地址中获取。

Abstract: In long-document question answering, a vision-language model can answer incorrectly even when the retrieved pages contain sufficient evidence. Rereading those pages with the original question gives the model little guidance about which fact to check. We introduce DoubleTake, a training-free framework for focused verification of visual evidence. An initial reading produces a candidate answer and a separate evidence record that guides retrieval for a second reading. A planner compares the two candidates and their supporting evidence to formulate a factual question. A verifier answers it from the source images without seeing either candidate or evidence record, and a reasoner uses this observation to resolve the original question. With a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DoubleTake achieves the highest overall accuracy among the evaluated methods on each of four benchmarks. With the same pages and number of model calls, asking a focused factual question yields higher accuracy on all four benchmarks than asking the original question again. Our code is available at https://github.com/small-code-cat/double_take.

</details>


### [25] [Speed-Variation Distillation for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17868)
*Sukit Seripanitkarn,Phonphrm Thawatdamrongkit,Supasorn Suwajanakorn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个生成框架，使用减少变异性的降解过程让多样化的图像向共享原型收缩，同时保持可识别的中间状态，通过生成先验指导的图像变形构建降噪轨迹，并引入分光操作将原型转化为更具多样性的样本，确保生成的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型生成图像时，其去噪路径会经过远离图像流形的损坏中间状态。本研究旨在探索生成整个轨迹期间能够始终保持可识别图像的生成方法，以提高生成质量并提供可控的速度-变异权衡。

Method: 该方法包含两个主要步骤：首先是减少变异性的降解过程，将多样化的图像逐步向共享原型收缩，同时保持中间状态的可识别性；第二步使用生成先验指导的图像变形技术来构建去噪路径，并通过分光操作将这些路径转化为多样性样本，最后通过调整初始噪声潜变量来解决反向过程中的歧义问题。

Result: 该方法成功让图像生成路径保持可识别性，确保了生成的图像在整个生成过程中都具有较高质量。此外，不同的初始噪声潜变量使生成过程具有可控性，能够实现速度和变异性的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种新颖的生成框架，通过巧妙的降解过程和图像变形技术，使得图像生成过程中始终保持可识别性，从而提高了生成图像的质量并提供了灵活性。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at image generation, their denoising trajectories pass through corrupted intermediate states far from the image manifold. We ask whether generation can instead proceed through recognizable images throughout the entire trajectory. We introduce a generative framework built around a variation-reducing degradation process that contracts diverse images toward a shared prototype while preserving recognizable intermediate states. Because such manifold traversal lacks a simple analytical form, we construct the trajectories using generative-prior-based image morphing. We then distill the reverse process into a diversification operator that transforms the prototype into increasingly varied samples. To resolve the one-to-many ambiguity of the reverse process, we associate each trajectory with its initial noise latent and apply perturbations to improve robustness against accumulated errors. At inference, every step yields a recognizable image, allowing early termination and enabling a controllable speed--variation trade-off.

</details>


### [26] [DySENSE: Dynamic Spatiotemporal ENergy Synthesis and Emulation with Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17878)
*Kailai Sun,Zijie Gao,Mingyi He,Can Rong,Alok Prakash,Baoshen Guo,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DySENSE 是一种结合静态城市卫星图像和动态月度建筑能耗地图的联合多模态生成人工智能框架。它能够生成具有高保真度的合成能耗数据，同时保持城市的静态形态。与现有方法相比，DySENSE提高了预测性能并减少了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市能耗对达成2050年净零目标和联合国可持续发展目标7和11至关重要，现有研究在数据和方法论上存在不足，DySENSE旨在通过结合静态和动态数据，填补这些空白，以更好地支持未来城市的能耗预测。

Method: DySENSE框架利用静态城市卫星图像和动态月度建筑能耗地图，通过解耦长期静态城市形态和短期动态能耗消费，在满足特定静态用地条件和动态月度及气候提示时，生成具有现实性和多样性的新静态城市影像以及动态月度能耗地图。

Result: DySENSE能够在最少25%的标注数据下生成注解的高保真度合成能耗数据，提升下游预测表现8% mIoU，并且显示了良好的能耗动态一致性。相比现有方法，DySENSE显著提高了预测性能（8.7%精度），并减少了预测误差（9%-18% CVRMSE 和 ASHRAE）。

Conclusion: DySENSE为能源高效城市、峰荷分析、建筑性能模拟和城市、能源及建筑科学中的动态需求响应提供了有效的基础。

Abstract: Cities account for over two-thirds of the world's energy and more than 70% of global carbon dioxide emissions. City energy is essential for achieving the 2050 net-zero target and the UN's Sustainable Development Goals 7 and 11. Although existing studies have made progress, they are limited by data and methodological gaps. Existing studies are largely predictive for existing cities rather than generative for future cities; fine-grained, aligned monthly dynamic energy data are scarce; and existing GenAI models struggle to generate dynamic urban functional monthly energy. We present DySENSE, a joint multimodal GenAI framework that synthesizes static urban satellite imagery together with spatially aligned, dynamic monthly building energy consumption maps. We decouple long-term static urban morphology from short-term dynamic energy consumption. By conditioning on static urban land use, DySENSE can generate realistic, diverse and new static urban imagery with aligned energy maps. By conditioning on dynamic monthly and climate prompts, DySENSE can jointly generate dynamic, monthly energy consumption maps while keeping the urban morphology static. Experiments across four cities (New York, Washington, Norfolk, Busan) demonstrated that DySENSE can generate annotated high-fidelity synthetic energy data using less than 25% labelled data, boosting downstream prediction performance by 8% mIoU, and achieving strong energy dynamic consistency. Compared to existing methods, DySENSE significantly improved prediction performance (8.7% accuracy) and reduced prediction error (9%-18% CVRMSE, ASHRAE). DySENSE offers an effective foundation for energy-efficient cities, peak-load analysis, building performance simulation and dynamic demand response for city, energy, and building science. The data and code: https://huggingface.co/datasets/skl24/MUSE2 and https://github.com/kailaisun/DySENSE

</details>


### [27] [Photogrammetric 3D Model-Based Animal Behavior Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17881)
*Olaf Hellwich,Moritz Heine,Patrik Reiske,Sole Traverso,Katharina Hohlbaum,Henning Sprekeler,Christa Thöne-Reineke,Lars Lewejohann,Roman Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段的方法，先重构3D人体和物体几何结构，再进行行为分类，以解决2D到3D逆问题带来的歧义，该方法在模拟和真实视频中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于图像序列的行为估计往往是端到端实现的，但这种方法可能无法很好地解决从2D到3D的逆问题所带来的歧义性。因此，本文提出了一种新的两阶段方法，旨在显式地展示逆问题的歧义并可能对其进行修正。

Method: 该方法包括两阶段：首先通过特定的技术（未详细描述）从视频中重建3D人体和物体的几何结构；其次，基于重建的3D数据进行行为分类。

Result: 在模拟交互鼠类的视频数据上，该方法验证了策略的有效性；并进一步将其扩展到现实世界视频中得到结果。

Conclusion: 这种两阶段的方法有助于解决行为估计中2D到3D逆问题带来的不确定性和潜在矫正这些不确定性提供了可能，从而提高行为估计的准确性。

Abstract: Video-based behavior estimation is often implemented end-to-end from image sequences. We advocate a two-stage alternative: reconstruct 3D body and object geometry before classifying behavior. This makes ambiguities of the ill-posed 2D-to-3D inverse problem explicit and potentially correctable. We demonstrate the strategy with simulated interacting mice and extend it to real video.

</details>


### [28] [See and Detect: Image-Driven Open-Vocabulary Multi-View 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17897)
*Yating Xu,Na Zhao,Chenhan Jiang,Qingwen Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图像的新型多视图室内3D检测框架，通过多视图整合提高3D盒子生成和语义标签生成的准确性，从而实现比现有最佳方法更快的速度和更优的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D检测方法依赖于点云完整性和局部外观，导致3D对象定位和开放词汇分类效果不佳。提出的方法通过多视图证据生成3D盒子和语义标签，提高鲁棒性和准确性。

Method: 首先从单个图像中发现对象候选，并通过多视图共识融合空间一致的预测以减少噪声。其次，引入基于上下文的语义学习策略，通过全图像上下文查询语义标签，进一步提升准确性。

Result: 在Scannet和ARKitScenes数据集上，与其他方法相比，提出的框架提供了优于现有最佳方法的定量和定性效果。在保持精度的同时速度提升超过500倍。

Conclusion: 此方法代表了多视图室内3D检测领域的一步改进，展示了在性能和速度上的显著提升。

Abstract: Recent open-vocabulary multi-view indoor 3D detection methods typically follow a point-cloud-driven pipeline, where pseudo 3D boxes are generated from reconstructed or estimated geometry and semantic labels are inferred from projected regions. However, such a design makes box generation dependent on the completeness of point clouds and restricts semantic learning to local appearance, causing inferior 3D object localization and open-vocabulary classification. In this paper, we present See and Detect, an image-driven framework that uses multi-view images as the primary evidence for 3D box and semantic label generation. For box generation, we first discover object candidates from individual images, enabling objects missing in point clouds to be recovered from visual evidence. However, single-view predictions are often noisy and redundant. To address this, we propose multi-view consensus box consolidation to fuse spatially consistent predictions across views, and box quality assessment to reduce the impact of boxes with partial object coverage. For semantic label generation, we introduce context-aware semantic learning, where semantic labels are queried from full image context, enabling more accurate semantic prediction. Extensive experiments on ScanNet and ARKitScenes demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art open-vocabulary multi-view 3D detection performance while running over 500$\times$ faster than the previous state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [29] [More Genes, Faster Inference: Efficient Large-Panel Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17899)
*Songming Zhang,Yuxiao Luo,Shifu Luo,Siran Chen,Shuquan Su,Jinyan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeneFlow 提出了一种简化且高效的方法，用于大型面板的空间基因表达预测，通过结合流匹配生成建模和结构化多模态融合，能够显著提高多种 ST 基准的性能，且生物意义明确，计算成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的空间转录组学预测方法复杂、性能有限且基因面板较小，GeneFlow 方法旨在解决这一问题，通过简化模型架构来提高大型基因面板的预测效率和准确性。

Method: GeneFlow 结合了流匹配生成建模和结构化多模态融合，利用组织学和基因标签器编码组织形态和跨基因间的作用。模型直接预测基因表达，而非中间的速度场，并采用基于流匹配的目标进行训练。

Result: GeneFlow 在多个 ST 基准测试中取得了最先进的性能，特别是在 1000 个基因的预测上获得了 56.8% 的提升。此外，它能够产出具有生物意义的斑点特征并大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: GeneFlow 提供了一种利用广泛可用的组织学图像进行下游生物发现的机会，通过简化建模和提高效率，特别是在临床环境中。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) prediction from histology aims to infer spatially resolved gene expression profiles directly from tissue images. This is a challenging problem that is currently often addressed with complicated model architectures, additional inputs, and complicated training procedures, while remaining limited to fixed, small gene panels. This raises a natural question: Is there an efficient method for large-panel ST prediction? Here, we present GeneFlow, a simpler yet effective framework for large-scale spatial gene expression prediction that unifies flow-matching generative modeling with structured multimodal fusion. Specifically, GeneFlow first encodes both histological morphology and cross-gene interactions in spatial spots through histology and gene tokenizers. The model directly predicts spatial gene expression, rather than an intermediate velocity field, while being trained with a velocity-based flow-matching objective. We show that, even without relying on heavy generative pipelines, GeneFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple ST benchmarks, with up to 56.8% gains in 1,000-gene prediction. Extensive experiments further show that GeneFlow produces biologically meaningful spot profiles while reducing computational cost by orders of magnitude, thereby improving efficiency and scalability, particularly in clinical settings. We believe GeneFlow provides an opportunity for leveraging widely available histology images for downstream biological discovery. Code is available at: https://github.com/ZZhangsm/GeneFlow

</details>


### [30] [AmbiSteer: Selectively Eliciting Ambiguity-Aware Responses in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17900)
*Monica Riedler,Samuele Antonelli,Barbara Plank,Raffaella Bernardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AmbiSteer 是一个轻量级框架，旨在通过调节和提示结合的方式减少视觉语言模型在模棱两可情境下的过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的视觉语言模型在处理模棱两可的问题时存在困难，往往倾向于单一解释，无法适当处理模棱两可和明确的问题。

Method: AmbiSteer 使用了激活调节和提示的结合方法来引导模型识别模棱两可的情况，并根据不同输入的模棱两可程度调整调节方向。

Result: 实验结果显示，与单纯激活调节相比，结合激活调节和提示显著增加了澄清和列举行为，但也在不确定的情况下激发了模棱两可的行为。结合方法将正确处理的比例从59.0%提高到77.0%。

Conclusion: AmbiSteer 代表了一种减轻视觉语言模型在不确定性环境中的过度自信的轻型方法，为未来工作的基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often exhibit overconfidence, providing firm answers even when visual evidence is insufficient. A prominent case is referential ambiguity in Visual Question Answering (VQA), where multiple entities in an image are plausible referents of a question. Addressing such cases requires recognizing when ambiguity is present: ambiguous questions can be handled by asking for clarification or enumerating plausible referents, while unambiguous questions should receive a direct answer. Current VLMs struggle with this distinction, often defaulting to a single interpretation. To investigate whether activation steering can serve as an inference-time mechanism to elicit such behaviors, we introduce AmbiSteer, a lightweight framework to selectively elicit ambiguity-aware behavior. Across three VLMs, we find that steering alone has limited and model-dependent effects, while prompting is generally more effective. Combining prompting and steering substantially increases clarification and enumeration behaviors, but also elicits them on unambiguous cases. AmbiSteer addresses this using a linear classifier to predict input ambiguity and condition the steering direction accordingly, increasing the proportion of inputs handled appropriately from 59.0% to 77.0% on average across models. Our work sets the ground for lightweight approaches to reduce model overconfidence in uncertain multimodal settings.

</details>


### [31] [Zoom In, Scale Out: Metric Depth Models Mistake Digital Zoom for Moving Closer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17903)
*Rabeya Noor,Khandoker Nosiba Arifin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过直接测试AI模型对数码变焦的应对能力，发现许多模型在估计深度时存在显著偏差，尤其是对于变焦图像的处理。研究证实，模型通常会错误地认为变焦等于相机靠近，导致深度估计不准确。研究人员提出了一种无需标签的微调方法，成功降低了深度变化的指数，使得模型能够更准确地处理未见过的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估当前深度估计模型在处理数码变焦时的准确性和局限性，研究人员设计了简便的测试方法，通过分析模型的反应参数来揭示深度估计错误的根本原因。

Method: 实验基于800张来自KITTI、NYUv2和DIODE的数据集的图像进行，通过数码变焦模拟不同场景的缩放效果，实验采用定量分析方法，包括计算α和β参数，来量化模型在面对变焦图片时的表现。此外，还提出了无标签微调策略以改善模型性能。

Result: 研究发现，常用的深度估计模型Depth Anything V2在面对数码变焦时会过度估计距离，具体表现为α值为0.95，而通过微调后该值可降至0.04。深度估计模型Depth Pro在使用真实相机数据后表现显著提升，α值接近0。同时，研究展示了无需标签的微调方法可以提高模型自一致性，减少对正常图像和未见过图像的处理差异。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了深度估计模型在处理数码变焦图像时的局限性，提出了一种有效的无标签优化策略。未来的工作可以进一步探索这些方法在多摄像头和复杂场景中的应用，以提高深度估计任务的整体表现。

Abstract: Many new AI models estimate how far away things are, in metres, from a single photo. To do this they must also work out what camera took the photo. We check whether they really do with a simple test: digital zoom, that is, cropping a photo and enlarging the crop back to full size. Zooming moves nothing in the scene, so a correct model should give the same distance for every point still in the picture. The test is free, exact and needs no labels. Two numbers describe a model's reaction: its depth changes like $s^{-α}$ (ideal $α=0$) and its focal-length estimate like $s^β$ (ideal $β=1$), for zoom factor $s$. On 800 images from KITTI, NYUv2 and DIODE, the widely used Depth Anything V2 has $α=0.95$: it treats a zoomed photo almost as if the camera had moved closer, because it judges distance mainly from how large things look. Models that also predict the camera remove only part of this error ($α\approx0.31$-$0.36$), because their focal estimate grows too little. Given the true camera, Depth Pro and DA3-Nested keep their depth ($α\approx0$), so the error lies in their camera estimate. The reason: a zoomed photo is, pixel for pixel, a normal photo of a scene squashed in depth, so size alone cannot tell the two apart, and a focal estimator trained by regression stays close to the cameras it saw in training. Undoing this needs no labels or training and makes Depth Pro pass the test ($α=0.04$) at almost no cost on normal photos. Controls, including a real optical zoom lens, rule out blur and resizing artefacts. Finally, label-free fine-tuning for self-consistency lowers $α$ to 0.05 on KITTI and 0.07 on NYUv2 once a "blurry means zoomed" shortcut is removed; we report what this costs on normal and unseen photos.

</details>


### [32] [Cracks in Embeddings: Robustness and Stability of JEPA-Based Chest X-Ray Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17905)
*Zahid Mansur,Kalpana Yadav,Deepak Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文测试了四种基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的胸部X射线编码器在六种扰动下的鲁棒性，包括黑盒对抗攻击和图像破坏等，结果显示纯干净数据训练的编码器在扰动环境中表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于自监督联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）模型在医学影像中的应用日益增多，但对扰动的鲁棒性理解不足，因此本文旨在测试这些模型在真实世界图像变化下的表现。

Method: 作者对78,468张NIH ChestX-ray14图像进行四种JEPA编码器的训练，并对每种编码器在六种测试中的表现进行了评估。其中包括两种黑盒对抗攻击（SPSA，边界攻击）、三种图像损坏（高斯噪声、亮度变化、对比度变化）和标签稀缺性测试。

Result: 在六种测试中，扰动导致各个模型的编码器级余弦相似度大幅下降，最严重的降幅达到了0.33，且AUROC最坏情况下降了0.12（边界攻击下I-JEPA）。对抗微调方法在多数情况下提升了嵌入一致性，尤其是I-JEPA在对抗微调后的提升最大，达到了+0.10 AUROC。而Vector-Quantized编码方法在遭受扰动时表现较差。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，编码器的扰动鲁棒性可以通过在干净和扰动数据上联合训练来提升，而无需依赖于编码器的离散瓶颈结构。

Abstract: Self-supervised Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models are increasingly used for medical imaging, however there is a limited understanding about robustness of these models toward real-world image changes. We test four JEPA-based chest X-ray encoders, trained on 78,468 NIH ChestX-ray14 images, under six tests: two black-box adversarial attacks (SPSA, Boundary Attack), three image corruptions (Gaussian noise, brightness shift, contrast shift), and a label-scarcity test, measuring embedding change and classification AUROC. These perturbations cause severe, model-dependent damage: encoder-level Cosine Similarity drops as low as 0.33, and AUROC falls by as much as 0.12 in the worst case (I-JEPA under the Boundary Test). We test two ways of training the small predictor on top of each frozen encoder to address this. Adversarial fine-tuning, trained on clean and perturbed images together, raises embedding consistency above 0.97 in nearly every case and improves AUROC, relative to the same perturbed baseline, for most models, with the largest gain of $+0.10$ on I-JEPA. A Vector-Quantized codebook method, trained only on clean images and evaluated on perturbed test images for the first time, instead degrades under distortion for all four models, most noticeably for I-JEPA ($-0.15$ AUROC on average), showing that its discrete bottleneck alone does not provide the robustness that comes from training on perturbed data directly.

</details>


### [33] [CardioTrace: Motion-Aware Cine Synthesis via Coupled Tracing and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17909)
*Zhi Chen,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CardioTrace 是一种从有限输入数据生成完整心脏 cine 序列的框架，同时隐式建模心脏运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床卡诺特MRI影像在时间分辨率、运动和心律不齐等方面受限，影响定量分析和后续处理。因此，需要一种方法来生成完整的心脏 cine 序列，既能提供清晰的时间分辨率，又能准确量化心脏运动。

Method: CardioTrace 结合了像素翻译支路和流动场支路，通过预测残差帧和估计时间分辨率变形场来生成帧序列。还加入了一个分割支路来监督关键相位的临床相关结构，并通过交叉支路一致性目标来保持解剖上合理。

Result: 实验表明，CardioTrace 在感知质量和时间连贯性方面优于 GAN 和扩散模型等代表性的基线模型，同时更好地保持了心脏形态。

Conclusion: CardioTrace 提供了一种有效的方法来自有限的输入数据创建心脏 cine 序列，提供了高质量的心脏运动跟踪，适用于多中心医疗数据。

Abstract: Cardiac cine MRI provides time-resolved visualization of cardiac motion and is essential for evaluating ventricular function and anatomy. In routine clinical acquisition, however, cine sequences are frequently temporally sparse or degraded by arrhythmia, motion, and scan-time constraints, which can hinder reliable quantification and downstream analysis. We present CardioTrace, a motion-aware cine synthesis framework that generates a full cardiac cine sequence from limited input while explicitly modeling and tracing cardiac motion. CardioTrace couples two complementary pathways: a pixel-translation branch that directly predicts cine frames in a residual formulation, and a flow-field branch that estimates time-resolved deformation fields from a reference phase to provide interpretable motion correspondences across frames. To further preserve anatomical plausibility, a segmentation branch supervises clinically meaningful cardiac structures at key phases, and a cross-branch consistency objective encourages the synthesized appearance dynamics to follow the explicit motion guidance, mitigating temporal drift and unrealistic shape transitions over long sequences. The framework also supports prompt-style conditioning to incorporate auxiliary patient or acquisition information, improving robustness across heterogeneous multi-center data. Experiments on public cardiac cine MRI datasets demonstrate that CardioTrace improves perceptual quality and temporal coherence over representative GAN- and diffusion-based baselines, while better preserving ventricular morphology throughout the cardiac cycle. \textit{Our codes are available after review}.

</details>


### [34] [Vessel2Brain: Edge-Aware Multi-Scale Flow Matching for 3D Brain Image Generation from Vessel Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17919)
*Zhi Chen,Tianyang Zhang,Theodoros N. Arvanitis,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Vessel2Brain的方法，该方法通过结合血管结构信息生成全3D大脑MRI切片，实现了高精度的结构对应和细节保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏的血管标签生成逼真的3D大脑MRI体积对医学图像增强、模拟及下游神经影像分析具有重要的临床价值。

Method: Vessel2Brain通过边意识多尺度条件流匹配框架将血管结构信息明确地纳入生成过程。引入多尺度流匹配策略以同时建模全局解剖连续性和细微局部细节，并设计边意识目标以加强血管边界在训练中的重要性。

Result: 在两个具有配对血管注释的公开3D大脑MRI数据集上，Vessel2Brain方法在重建准确性、结构一致性以及分布现实性方面均显著优于现有的生成模型。

Conclusion: 这项研究提出了一种创新的方法来解决血管引导下的全3D大脑MRI图像合成问题，并展示了其在实际应用中的优越性能。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D brain MRI volumes from sparse anatomical priors is a highly ill-posed yet clinically valuable problem, with important applications in data augmentation, simulation, and downstream neuroimaging analysis. In this work, we investigate the challenging task of synthesising full 3D brain MRI volumes conditioned solely on vascular label maps. While vascular structures provide strong anatomical and topological cues, they occupy only a small fraction of the brain volume and offer no direct information about surrounding tissue appearance. To address this challenge, we propose Vessel2Brain, an edge-aware multi-scale conditional flow matching framework that explicitly incorporates vascular structure information into the generative process. Our approach formulates vessel-guided brain image synthesis as a conditional continuous flow, enabling stable training and flexible generation while preserving structural correspondence. A multi-scale flow matching strategy is introduced to jointly model global anatomical coherence and fine-grained local details, with auxiliary velocity predictions providing additional supervision across spatial resolutions. Furthermore, we design an edge-aware objective that emphasises vascular boundaries during training, encouraging anatomically faithful tissue synthesis around vessel structures. We evaluate Vessel2Brain on two public 3D brain MRI datasets with paired vascular annotations. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative models in terms of reconstruction accuracy, structural consistency, and distributional realism.

</details>


### [35] [Token2Tile: Token-Wise Sparse Attention with Tile Execution through a Low-Rank Space for Video DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17930)
*Xuecheng Wu,Aichen Feng,Jinyan Chen,Qingjie Zhao,Julien Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Token2Tile 提出了一种无需训练的稀疏注意力算子，通过结合查询特定的评分和残差以及分块执行，实现了在 MiniMax-H3 和 Wan2.1-14B 上相较于密集 FlashAttention-3 1.48-1.54 倍的端到端加速，并且其 VBench 分数仅差 0.5 分。在 H3 上，与 SOL 和 SubBlock 相比，它在 PSNR 方面分别提高了 5.5-6.8 dB；而对于 Wan，在相同速度下，它超出了 SOL 4.6-4.8 dB，但运行速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决注意力机制在视频扩散变换器中建模空时依赖关系时的计算成本问题，同时考虑到 GPU 并行处理的特点，以提高推理效率并保持准确性。

Method: Token2Tile 将查询特定的评分和残差结合分块执行。具体而言，通过在线低秩空间直接从查询和键中计算得到，优化了聚类、评分和残差近似过程，从而实现了跨查询集群的精确工作分配，并将所选键打包进 GPU 分块。

Result: Token2Tile 在 MiniMax-H3 和 Wan2.1-14B 上分别实现了 1.48-1.54 倍的端到端加速，且 VBench 分数与密集模型接近。在 H3 上，Token2Tile 的 PSNR 在与 SOL 和 SubBlock 的比较中分别提高了 5.5-6.8 dB；在 Wan 上，其 PSNR 超过 SOL 4.6-4.8 dB，同时速度更快。

Conclusion: 这一研究提出了一种新颖的稀疏注意力算子 Token2Tile，显著提升了模型的推理速度并保持了较高的性能。

Abstract: Attention is essential for modeling spatio-temporal dependencies in video diffusion transformers, but its quadratic cost makes it a major inference bottleneck. Its high sparsity offers an opportunity to accelerate generation by focusing computation on important interactions. However, accuracy and execution favor different granularities: each query attends to its own key tokens, whereas GPU kernels work on tiles. We present Token2Tile, a training-free sparse attention operator that combines per-query scoring and a per-query residual with tile execution. An online low-rank space, computed directly from queries and keys to preserve attention logits, makes clustering, scoring, and residual approximation efficient. Token2Tile unites query-specific selections to allocate exact work across query clusters, then packs the selected keys into GPU tiles. On MiniMax-H3 and Wan2.1-14B, Token2Tile delivers 1.48-1.54x end-to-end speedup over dense FlashAttention-3 with VBench scores within 0.5 points of dense. At comparable speeds on H3, it improves PSNR over SOL and SubBlock by 5.5-6.8 dB; on Wan, it exceeds SOL by 4.6-4.8 dB while running faster.

</details>


### [36] [Domain Adaptation Based Pipeline for Character Classification and Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17952)
*Florent Imbert,Simon Corbillé,Hui Han,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无监督领域适应管道，分别在源数据和目标域风格数据上预训练和训练专家模型，以解决字符分类和手写文本识别中由于有限标记数据的问题。该方法通过在源和目标域之间对齐特征分布，提高了模型在不同数据集上的泛化能力，并取得新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决字符分类和手写文本识别任务中由于有限标记数据导致的训练效果不佳问题，同时最大化利用大量未标记数据的潜力。

Method: 该方法包含三个阶段：首先，基于源数据预训练专家模型学习通用表示；其次，通过无监督领域适应利用目标域的未标记数据，使模型适应目标域的特性；最后，基于目标域样式的源数据进一步训练模型。

Result: 该方法在字符识别和历史手写行识别任务上进行了评估，特别是在没有使用目标数据标签的情况下，在Safran-MNIST-DLS数据集上达到了76.17%的宏F1分数，为每个特定的READ 2018数据集取得了可竞争的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明通过无监督领域适应管道，可以在有限标记数据和大量未标记数据中有效提高模型的泛化性能，并且在权威竞赛数据集上取得了新纪录，展示了其在实际应用中的灵活性和有效性。

Abstract: Character classification and handwritten text recognition, particularly in specialized tasks, often suffer from limited labeled data, while a large amount of unlabeled data remains unused. To address this challenge, we propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation-based pipeline to reduce the distribution gap between source and target domains. Our pipeline follows three main steps. In the first step, we pretrained an expert model on source data, to learn a strong general representation. In the second step, we perform unsupervised domain adaptation to leverage unlabeled target data, adapting the model to the specific characteristics of the target domain. In the third step, we trained the expert model on source data in the target style. By aligning the feature distributions between the source and target, our method enables the model to generalize effectively across diverse datasets. The unsupervised training increases the computation time only during training not in inference. We evaluate the pipeline on both character-level and historical handwritten line recognition tasks, demonstrating its flexibility. We establish a new state-of-the-art on the Safran-MNIST-DLS dataset defined in Track 2 of the DAGECC competition with 76.17% Macro F1-score and achieve competitive results on each of the specific datasets of READ 2018 without using the labels of the target data. Code is available at https://github.com/simon-corbi/DRANet-for-classification-and-htr

</details>


### [37] [nnAEU: a Simple and Efficient CNN-Autoencoder for Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17956)
*Edgard Dabier,Christophe Kervazo,Pietro Gori,Florence Tupin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 nnAEU 的轻量级 CNN 自编码器，该模型通过从 UNMamba 中移除 Mamba 组件进行剪枝得到，具有少于 UNMamba 10 倍的参数量，同时在准确性上优于其他基于变换器和 GAN 的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
