*Zhongmou He,Yee Man Choi,Kexun Zhang,Jiabao Ji,Junting Zhou,Dejia Xu,Ivan Bercovich,Aidan Zhang,Yixiao Zeng,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为HARDTESTGEN的方法，用于生成高质量的测试案例来验证大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的代码。通过构建一个包含26600个问题和高质量合成测试的综合算法编程数据集HARDTESTS，该方法在精度上比现有测试提高了11.22个百分点，并提升了LLM后续训练的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于目前可靠的验证器难以开发，特别是对于代码生成任务，HARDTESTGEN旨在生成高质量的测试案例以提高LLM输出的准确性，并增强其代码生成性能。

Method: HARDTESTGEN方法通过手动精选和自动合成生成高质量的算法编程问题及其测试案例，构建了名为HARDTESTS的专项数据集。

Result: HARDTESTGEN测试在验证LLM生成代码的准确性上表现出显著提升（在精度上为+11.22个百分点）。使用HARDTESTS验证器进行下游训练后（如拒绝抽样、强化学习等），语言模型的代码生成性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了HARDTESTGEN方法的设计与实现，以及其对大型语言模型代码生成效果的积极影响，同时该算法编程数据集和生成管道已开源。

Abstract: Verifiers provide important reward signals for reinforcement learning of large language models (LLMs). However, it is challenging to develop or create reliable verifiers, especially for code generation tasks. A well-disguised wrong solution program may only be detected by carefully human-written edge cases that are difficult to synthesize automatically. To address this issue, we propose HARDTESTGEN, an approach to synthesize high-quality test cases for algorithmic coding problems. We curate a comprehensive algorithmic programming dataset HARDTESTS with 26.6k problems and high-quality synthetic tests. Compared with existing tests, HARDTESTGEN tests demonstrate significantly higher accuracy in verifying LLM-generated code (+11.22 percentage points in precision, the percentage of actually correct code within the predicted correct ones). We also show that downstream post-training -- including rejection sampling and reinforcement learning (RL) -- using HARDTESTS verifier results in improved performance of LLM code generation. We open-source our dataset and synthesis pipeline at https://leililab.github.io/HardTests/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [CHARMING: Parallel Edge AI Acceleration Across Tensor Processing and In-Memory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17785)
*Md Omar Faruque,Jingang Jin,João Paulo C. de Lima,Shouzhi Fang,William C. Tegge,Peipei Zhou,Qinru Qiu,Alex K. Jones*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHARMING 提出了一种混合边缘 AI 架构，结合张量处理单元和片上内存计算单元，通过精度和量化感知的分区优化模型执行，提高了预填充和解码性能，并减少了每层的能量消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘 AI 加速器由于数据移动导致效率低下，CHARMING 旨在通过结合片上内存计算来优化模型执行，减少不必要的数据移动，从而提高能效。

Method: CHARMING 使用精度和量化感知的分区策略，将注意力头分布在张量处理单元和片上内存计算单元之间，根据模型架构、序列长度和精度平衡执行，同时在 AIE 投影阶段整合量化，进一步减少数据移动。

Result: 实验结果表明，CHARMING 在硬件上实现了 AMD Versal VCK5000 和 Alveo U200 平台之间的端到端验证，对于不同的精度提供 LLM 和视频生成模型的评估，显示了性能和能效的显著提升。

Conclusion: CHARMING 在 AIE 加速器的基础上，通过集成内存计算加速器显著提升了性能和能效，特别是在 32 位浮点基线下保持了类似水平的准确性。

Abstract: Edge AI increasingly relies on specialized accelerators for low-latency inference under tight resource and energy constraints, yet their efficiency remains limited by data movement. Prior work, CHARM, mitigates this overhead by overlapping data movement with computation. We observe that in-memory computing can further supplement tensor processing as a parallel accelerator while eliminating certain data movements entirely.
  We present CHARMING, a heterogeneous edge AI architecture that uses accuracy- and quantization-aware partitioning to distribute and concurrently execute inference workloads across tensor processing and in-memory computation. CHARMING partitions attention heads between an AIE array and an in-DRAM compute unit, balancing execution according to model architecture, sequence length, and precision while selecting heads to minimize accuracy degradation. CHARMING also integrates quantization into the AIE projection stage to further reduce data movement. We validate CHARMING end-to-end in hardware on AMD Versal VCK5000 and Alveo U200 platforms and evaluate LLM and video-generation models across multiple precisions using measured hardware results and simulation of the latest in-memory compute hardware.
  CHARMING improves prefill performance by up to 23.6 percent and decode performance by up to 44 percent over AIE-only acceleration. With int4 x int4 fixed-point in-memory computation, CHARMING reduces per-layer energy by up to 7.6 percent and improves energy-delay product by up to 24.7 percent, while maintaining accuracy similar to a 32-bit floating-point baseline.

</details>


### [112] [Elasticy: A Runtime-Reconfigurable Architecture for Dynamic Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17798)
*Konstantin J Hossfeld,Nathan Sobotka,Gina Sohn,Tun-Yu Chang,Kunle Olukotun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Elasticy 提出了一种动态可重构数据流加速器，它利用流和瓦片抽象来减少动态支持的开销，并通过编码动态形状来利用数据依赖的张量形状动态性，减少外部数据流量和浪费的计算周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态配置数据流加速器在处理具有数据依赖性、非均匀和动态张量形状的不规则张量工作负载时表现出明显的性能和效率下降。研究者提出了Elasticy，以应对这些挑战。

Method: Elasticy 利用流嵌入的重配置信息，触发细粒度的运行时调度决策，并引入了一种新型分布式链接流内存 (DLSM)，该内存更有效地使用了片上内存。

Result: 在 Llama 3-8B 解码层上，Elasticy 达到了 92.6% 的顶线性能，而 H100 的性能则为 69.7%。与最佳评估缓存配置相比，DLSM 可减少总外部数据流量多达 61.4%。实时调度器重叠了相关核，对核对对提高了高达 1.42 倍。与传统空间加速器相比，Elasticy 在整个层上的面积延迟产品提高了6.34到31.67倍。

Conclusion: 通过减少操作开销和优化计算资源利用，Elasticy 显着提高了不规则张量任务处理的性能和能效。

Abstract: Reconfigurable Dataflow Accelerators (RDAs) have demonstrated high performance and energy efficiency for dense, regular tensor applications. However, conventional RDAs with static configurations show dramatically reduced performance and efficiency on irregular tensor workloads with data-dependent, non-uniform, dynamic tensor shapes. We propose Elasticy, a dynamically reconfigurable dataflow accelerator that employs streaming and tile abstractions to minimize the overhead of dynamic support. By encoding dynamic shapes into streams using rank-variable expressions, Elasticy exploits data-dependent tensor shape dynamism to minimize off-chip data traffic and wasted compute cycles. Elasticy employs stream-embedded reconfiguration information to trigger fine-grained runtime scheduling decisions. Elasticy also introduces a novel distributed linked streaming memory (DLSM) that more effectively uses on-chip memory to minimize off-chip data traffic and support dynamically sized tensors.
  Both reconfiguration and shape dynamism incur minimal overhead in Elasticy: reconfiguration occurs infrequently at the granularity of a stream of tiles, while rank-variable expressions enable lightweight handling of dynamic dimensions. On a Llama 3-8B decode layer, Elasticy achieves 92.6% of its roofline performance, compared to 69.7% on an H100. On evaluated GEMMs, the DLSM reduces total off-chip traffic by up to 61.4% relative to the best evaluated cache configuration, with no additional burden of programmer-managed memory. Elasticy's distributed runtime scheduler overlaps dependent kernels, speeding up kernel pairs by up to 1.42x. Programs can also run in both space and time: each phase of a layer's graph is pipelined spatially, while phases fold onto the same fabric over time. Compared to a traditional spatial accelerator, Elasticy achieves a 6.34x-31.67x improved area delay product on full layers.

</details>


### [113] [SIMTurn: Lightweight Divergent Subgroup Scheduling for Intra-Warp Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17827)
*Nikhil Rout,Joe Samuel Rosh,Blaise Tine*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SIMTurn 通过在潜在阻塞循环中插入编译器插入的 yield 指令和有限的可恢复子组池，让发散子组之间能够前进，同时保留基于栈的重新汇聚。与独立线程调度相比，SIMTurn 的调度器面积开销相对较小，但性能更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决使用基于栈的重新汇聚时发散路径间的死锁问题，同时减少独立线程调度引入的额外硬件成本。

Method: SIMTurn 结合了编译器插入的 yield 指令和有限的可恢复子组池，调度器允许等待中的子组向能够前进的兄弟子组让出。

Result: SIMTurn 相对于基线 IPDOM 的调度器面积开销仅增加 17-24%，而独立线程调度增加 39-91%。对于不需要跨子组前进的工作负载，SIMTurn 性能保持在 IPDOM 附近。在具有真跨子组并行性的内核中，SIMTurn 的性能可与独立线程调度相当，甚至快 2.7 倍。

Conclusion: SIMTurn 为实现高效的可重汇合并允许发散子组之间进程提供了一种轻量级的编译器-硬件机制。

Abstract: Stack-based reconvergence enables efficient SIMT execution with a shared warp program counter and compact control-flow state. However, its fixed serialization of divergent paths can deadlock programs that rely on intra-warp synchronization: a spinning subgroup may prevent a resource-holding sibling from executing. Independent Thread Scheduling (ITS) enables finer-grained progress through per-thread execution state, but introduces additional hardware cost. We present SIMTurn, a lightweight compiler-hardware mechanism that enables forward progress among divergent subgroups while retaining stack-based reconvergence. SIMTurn combines a compiler-inserted yield instruction at potentially blocking loops with a bounded pool of resumable subgroups. The scheduler allows a waiting subgroup to yield to a sibling that can make progress, without requiring independent program counters for every thread. We implement SIMTurn in the compiler, simulator, and RTL on the open-source Vortex GPGPU and evaluate it against baseline IPDOM and per-thread ITS schemes. Across the evaluated GPU thread configurations, SIMTurn introduces only 17-24% scheduler-area overhead relative to IPDOM, compared with 39-91% for ITS, and stays within 8% of IPDOM on workloads that never need cross-subgroup progress. On kernels with genuine cross-subgroup parallelism, SIMTurn tracks ITS closely and is up to 2.7x faster than a software-serialized IPDOM baseline.

</details>


### [114] [DiffusionSSD: Achieving Extreme Visual Compression with Dual-stage Diffusion and Cross-level Near-Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17908)
*Kuai Yu,Wen Li,Shengwen Liang,Jie Zhang,Ying Wang,Huawei Li,Xin Yao,Renhai Chen,Gong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DiffusionSSD 提出了一种基于算法和架构协同设计的框架，通过 Dual-stage Diffusion 和 Cross-level Near Data Processing 增强了边缘设备上极端视觉数据压缩的性能，减少了计算成本并提高了编码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对边缘设备存储资源受限和视觉数据压缩需求，传统压缩算法表现不佳，Inspired by 差分编解码的强大感知质量保持能力，同时解决现有差分编解码的高计算成本和设备内部架构干扰现有 AI 应用的问题。

Method: 算法方面，引入了 Dual-stage Diffusion，一种轻量级解决方案，通过分割和单步差分减少计算成本；架构方面，提出了 Cross-level Near Data Processing 架构，将计算卸载到数据端，减少视觉数据移动，不需进行 SoC 重设计；系统层面，提出了一种混合工作负载感知调度策略，以增加系统的执行效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，Dual-stage Diffusion 在保持可感知质量的同时，计算成本最多可减少 92.7%。在编码吞吐量方面，相比于 NPU+DRAM 和近数据处理设计（如 SmartSSD），cUFS+PIM 分别提高了 2.32 倍和 2.91 倍。

Conclusion: DiffusionSSD 成功地提高了边缘设备上极端视觉数据压缩的性能，通过算法和架构的协同设计，有效解决了传统压缩方法和差分编解码的挑战。

Abstract: Growing visual data overwhelms storage on resource-constrained edge devices, making compression essential. Traditional codecs offer inadequate rate-distortion performance, motivating the exploration of diffusion codecs. Diffusion codecs face two limitations: (1) At the algorithm level, diffusion decoders preserve perceptual quality under extreme compression, but require substantial computation. (2) At the architecture level, in NPU-centered devices, neural codecs occupy the NPU, interfering with other AI applications. Meanwhile, the complex codec pipeline incurs long I/O paths, leading to additional overhead and energy cost.
  To address these issues, we propose DiffusionSSD, a cross-level near-data processing framework for extreme visual data compression on edge devices, using an algorithm-architecture co-design method. Specifically, (1) at algorithm level, we present Dual-stage Diffusion, a lightweight solution tailored for diffusion codecs that employs patchification and single-step diffusion, reducing computation cost while preserving perceptual quality; (2) at architecture level, we introduce a Cross-level Near Data Processing architecture that integrates the proposed computational universal flash storage (cUFS) and LPDDR-PIM, enabling codecs to be entirely offloaded to data side while reducing visual-data movement and avoiding SoC redesign; (3) at system level, combining both the algorithm and the underlying architecture, we propose a Patch-Workload co-aware scheduling scheme to enable more efficient system-level execution. Experiments show that Dual-stage Diffusion maintains comparable perceptual quality while reducing computation cost by up to 92.7%. Compared with NPU+DRAM and near-data-processing design such as SmartSSD, cUFS+PIM improves coding throughput by 2.32 times and 2.91 times on average, respectively.

</details>


### [115] [OpenLayout: An Open and Agentic-Driven Platform for Holistic VLSI Physical Design and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18517)
*Qiufeng Li,Sizhe Tang,Quan Cheng,Zirui Wang,Wantong Li,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为OpenLayout的平台，该平台利用多智能体协作和嵌入式设计知识，实现了自我迭代的物理设计自动化优化。OpenLayout使用大语言模型结合人类设计经验，以高效自动化的方式处理设计空间优化问题，实验结果显示其性能显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理设计自动化工具在面对日益复杂的设计空间时表现不佳，设计流程高度依赖人类专家的工作，消耗大量工程资源。为此，作者提出了一种新方法，利用大语言模型（LLMs）结合人的设计经验来实现高效和自动化的物理设计空间优化。

Method: 作者使用了一个名为OpenLayout的平台，该平台基于多智能体系统和嵌入式设计知识，能够进行自我迭代的设计优化。OpenLayout通过利用大语言模型的强大推理能力，自然地整合了人类专家以自然语言表达的经验，从而在物理设计自动化中实现高效优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用OpenLayout进行物理设计自动化优化时，相较基线方法，性能提高了约20%的准确度。OpenLayout还提供了一个名为OpenLayout-Bench的基准测试框架，用于评估LLMs在物理设计自动化中的能力。

Conclusion: 本文展示了OpenLayout平台在利用多智能体系统和人类设计知识进行高效设计优化方面的潜力，并通过实验验证了其优越性，未来有望进一步推广至实际工业应用中。

Abstract: Physical design automation of very large-scale integrated circuits (VLSI) has made significant strides, progressing from single-stage placement and routing toward increasingly integrated full-flow implementation. Yet, existing approaches struggle with the vast design space optimization (DSO) arising from diverse technology nodes, circuit designs, and varied constraints. The substantial runtime of physical-design tools further exacerbates this challenge, leaving industrial workflows heavily dependent on experienced engineers to navigate the design space and achieve power, performance, and area (PPA) targets, incurring significant engineering effort.
  We argue that large language models (LLMs) with strong reasoning capabilities, enriched with distilled human design expertise, offer a promising path toward efficient and automated DSO. Unlike traditional algorithms, LLMs can interpret complex design contexts and naturally incorporate human expertise expressed in natural language for reasoning, acting, observing, and adapting throughout the design process, a capability that conventional optimization methods cannot exploit. To this end, we present OpenLayout, an agentic platform that leverages multi-agent collaboration with embedded design knowledge to enable self-iterative DSO. We further introduce OpenLayout-Bench, a benchmark targeting advanced technology nodes with commercial EDA tools to systematically evaluate physical design automation capabilities of LLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that OpenLayout achieves state-of-the-art performance, with approximately 20% accuracy improvement over baseline methods. Our work is open-sourced at https://github.com/fenggev56/OpenLayout

</details>


### [116] [Mutable Inference: Architecting LLM Accelerators for Inference-Time Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18523)
*Yimin Gao,Liangtao Dai,Melika Morsali,Junting Huo,Mircea Stan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: LWS架构通过利用注意力操作中的闲置矩阵操作能力和部分延迟权重更新，实现在推理加速器中同时进行预测和学习，从而提高LLM模型的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的推理时学习方法虽然能在固定大小的窗口内匹配全上下文注意力的效果，但会增加额外的计算成本。为了克服这一问题，作者提出了Learning While Serving (LWS)架构，该架构能够在推理过程中的关键延迟部分和灵活的截止时间部分之间共享计算硬件，从而提高模型效率。

Method: LWS架构通过硬件管理的逐层权重版本化技术，允许学习过程在计算过程中发布更新的同时，读取较旧的版本。此外，LWS还引入了可恢复缩减块和专门的软化-梯度单元，以缓解向量瓶颈。这些创新方法有助于平衡预测和学习的计算需求，实现高效利用闲置推理能力的目标。

Result: 实验数据显示，在模型数据中心和边缘NPU上，LWS在维持99.3%的学习质量优势的同时，隐藏了学习过程。在9个模型上，LWS的吞吐量比串行执行提高了1.6倍，比最好之前的联合执行架构提高了1.3倍。此外，RTL综合表明，LWS架构仅增加了1.5%的核心面积，没有添加额外的MAC阵列。

Conclusion: LWS架构展示了推理时学习作为一种新的计算工作负载的可能性，特别是对于未来长时间序列模型的加速器设计而言。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs must retain growing histories, but full attention makes each new token increasingly expensive. Inference-time learning (ITL) offers another approach. The model keeps training on the context during inference, retaining what it learns in its weights. Recent work shows that ITL can match full-context attention quality while attending only to a recent fixed-size window. This bounds attention costs but adds Learning (backward computation and weight updates) alongside Prediction (the forward pass) costs. Inference accelerators assume fixed weights, while training accelerators target complete training steps.
  This work identifies two opportunities. Attention's non-matrix operations leave enough idle matrix capacity for Learning, while selected layers can tolerate delayed weight updates with little quality loss. We exploit both with Learning While Serving (LWS), an inference accelerator architecture that shares compute hardware between latency-critical Prediction and deadline-flexible Learning. Hardware-managed per-layer weight versioning allows Learning to publish updates while in-flight computations read older versions. Resumable reduction blocks let Learning kernels span fragmented idle windows and yield to Prediction, while dedicated softmax-gradient units relieve vector contention. On modeled datacenter and edge NPUs, LWS hides Learning within Prediction, sustaining Prediction-only throughput while preserving 99.3% of fully fresh Learning's quality benefit for TTT-E2E 3B at 128K tokens. Across nine models, LWS achieves 1.6x the throughput of serial execution and 1.3x that of the best prior co-execution architecture. RTL synthesis shows that LWS adds 1.5% to NPU core area without another MAC array. By efficiently turning idle inference capacity into learning capacity, LWS establishes inference-time learning as a first-class workload for future LLM accelerators.

</details>


### [117] [EventSpikeSeg: Memory-Aware Mixed-Signal Architecture for Event-Camera Spiking Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18524)
*Amir Mohammad Khadem Hosseini,Sattar Mirzakuchaki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: EventSpikeSeg是一种结合驻留电容记忆和共享模拟处理的嵌入式架构，实现事件摄像头分割，在180nm CMOS工艺中具有25个种群的脉冲编码-解码器，通过详细的SPICE校准行为前向仿真，获得了接近传统软件参考的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是开发一种解决事件摄像头分割问题的高效嵌入式架构，特别关注系统功耗和能量效率，以满足低功耗应用的需求。

Method: 该架构结合了驻留电容记忆与共享模拟处理，包含25个种群的脉冲编码-解码器，具有143,824个突触权重，需要30,976,000个膜状态。系统采用了模块化设计，并进行了22模块家族和74个操作点的测试，使用了混合信号仿真的方法，通过软件和硬件层次的对比分析潜在的偏差。

Result: 实验结果显示，该架构在440x640分辨率下的功耗为41.711微瓦，每个电容器的偏置时间为1.4微妙时的能量消耗为3.338pJ，长时间保持选择态仅存在0.531微伏的漂移。详细的SPICE校准行为前向仿真表明，在包含完整数据集的仿真中，该架构的性能达到了24.26%的平均交并比，与参考软件PyTorch的26.22%接近。

Conclusion: 研究表明EventSpikeSeg架构在处理事件摄像头分割时具有良好的能效和性能，但在实际物理系统中的能耗和可靠性验证还未完成。

Abstract: Event-camera segmentation requires persistent neural state despite sparse input events, making memory organization a central constraint on embedded implementation. We present EventSpikeSeg, a mixed-signal architecture combining resident capacitor memory with shared analog processing in GF180MCU 180-nm CMOS. Its 25-population spiking encoder-decoder has 143,824 synaptic weights but requires 30,976,000 membrane states at 440 x 640 resolution. The mapping specifies chronological 5-ms model updates, digital event/address services and 64-state capacitor banks. Characterization covers 22 module families and 74 operating points, distinguishing transistor simulations from digital-library estimates. Nominal results include 41.711 microwatts biased neuron-core power, 3.338-pJ positive terminal energy for a 1.4-microsecond cell read, and 0.531-mV selected-state drift over a 5-ms extracted-tile hold. Commit and coupling errors are reported separately. The software checkpoint records 27.976% mean intersection-over-union on a held-out six-class DSEC split; this is not hardware accuracy. Component layouts and simulation evidence quantify local implementation costs and expose storage replication and service scheduling as scaling constraints. A detailed SPICE-calibrated behavioral forward simulation of one full-resolution DSEC frame achieves 24.26% mean intersection-over-union, versus 26.22% for the matched PyTorch reference, using a compact 5-fF bank variant and external count/voltage calibration. Physical full-network operation, system energy and mismatch robustness remain unverified.

</details>


### [118] [WaferTetris: Efficient Freeform Mapping on Wafer-Scale Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18527)
*Dayou Du,Congjie He,Andrew Boutros,Luo Mai,Vaughn Betz,Jianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: WaferTetris通过自由映射策略提高了计算单元和内存资源的利用率，实现了LLM解码等场景下的高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模板映射方法限制了映射设计空间，导致计算和内存资源利用率不足。

Method: WaferTetris 使用了一种类似于 Tetris 的策略，以自由方式填充计算任务。它开发了一种基于递归的吞吐量模型来捕捉分支和联合依赖性，以指导空间并行性，并建立了具有按颜色门电路的架构模型来允许 FPGA 预置算法强制执行 WSE 路由约束。

Result: 在 Cerebras WSE-3 上，WaferTetris 在 LLM 解码中的系统吞吐量比基于模板的自动映射基线提高了 4.42 倍。此外，它为 LLaMA3.1-8B 支持的上下文长度增加了 1.8 倍，达到了 80,000，同时在匹配的上下文长度下提供相当或更高的每用户吞吐量，最高为 1.10 倍。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何通过自由映射来优化 wafer-scale 加速器的性能，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Wafer-scale accelerators offer massive compute capacity through hundreds of thousands of processing elements (PEs) interconnected across a single wafer, but realizing this potential requires efficient application mapping. Their scale makes programming these systems and mapping applications onto them challenging. Existing approaches rely on template-based mappings to maintain productivity, but these templates restrict the mapping design space. In particular, they impose fixed geometric layouts over the PE grid, often leaving compute and memory resources underutilized.
  In this work, we present WaferTetris, a system that removes these geometric restrictions through freeform mapping. Its Tetris-like strategy flexibly packs computation across the wafer, improving PE utilization and usable memory capacity while preserving PE-task code. We develop a recurrence-based throughput model that captures branch-and-join dependencies to guide spatial parallelism, and an architecture model with per-color gates that enable existing FPGA place-and-route algorithms to enforce WSE routing constraints.
  We evaluate WaferTetris on Cerebras WSE-3 using LLM decoding and scientific stencil benchmarks. For LLM decoding, it achieves up to $4.42\times$ the system throughput of a template-based automatic mapping baseline on the same fabric size. Compared with the manually optimized WaferLLM, WaferTetris supports $1.8\times$ the context length for LLaMA3.1-8B (80k versus 44k), while delivering equal or higher per-user throughput at matched context lengths, up to $1.10\times$.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [PerfArena: Ranking LLMs by Code Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18030)
*Rui Rua,Hailemariam Mersha,Joao Paulo Fernandes,Karim Ali*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: PERFARENA 是一个开源、可重用的平台，用于评估生成代码的性能，涵盖多种编程语言和比赛。它提供了性能、正确性和能耗等方面的数据，有助于开发者选择合适的生成式人工智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对 LLM 生成代码的评估主要集中在正确性上，而忽略了性能和资源消耗等因素，PERFARENA 的动机是填补这一空白，为开发者提供一个全面的评估平台。

Method: PERFARENA 通过在类 Unix 主机上执行 LLM 生成的代码，报告了代码的正确性、延迟、峰值内存和能耗等性能指标，使用插件式的评估和测量工具来实现灵活的评估方法。

Result: PERFARENA 评估了来自 13 种先进 LLM 的 9,455 个正确且测量过的解决方案，跨越 7 种编程语言。got-6-astra 在两个竞赛中的表现尤为突出，显示出其在延迟、能耗和峰值内存使用方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: PERFARENA 通过提供全面的性能数据和可插拔的评估工具，填补了现有 LLM 评估的不足，有助于开发者根据具体需求选择合适的生成式 AI 服务。

Abstract: Industry now relies on LLMs to write a large share of production code. Although that code is mostly, but not always, correct, correctness alone does not determine whether it is fit for repeated and extensive use, which depends on what the code costs in time, memory and energy. Most code-generation benchmarks nonetheless evaluate correctness in isolation, and most performance-oriented benchmarks cover few languages and rarely measure energy consumption. To address this gap, we present PERFARENA, an open-source, reproducible arena that ranks LLMs by the performance of their generated code across multiple languages and competitions. PERFARENA executes the solutions of each LLM on a Unix-like host and reports correctness together with latency, peak memory, and energy consumption. Correctness oracles, performance oracles, profilers, and code-generation workflows are pluggable in PERFARENA, enabling the integration of new judging backends and measurement tools without changing the pipeline. We evaluate PERFARENA on two competitions: CLBGComp, 10 compute-intensive benchmarks from the Computer Language Benchmarks Game, and LeetCodeComp, 99 LeetCode problems whose accepted solutions differ measurably in latency. Currently, PERFARENA holds 9,455 correct and measured solutions from 13 state-of-the-art LLMs across 7 programming languages. Across both competitions, got-6-astra is the only LLM that no other LLM beats significantly in latency, energy consumption, or peak memory, solving 99.7% of LeetCodeComp and 100% of CLBGComp. All PERFARENA leaderboards and measured solutions are publicly available, providing a continuously updated reference that helps developers choose an LLM per language and metric, instead of relying only on correctness rates or pricing.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [When Should LLM Agents Forget? Memory Filtering Under Query-Critical Resolver Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17338)
*Samarth Vinayaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了LLM记忆系统中删除过时信息（stale facts）的硬性过滤步骤对模型下游准确度的影响。研究发现，在不同的模型和可靠性水平下，这种过滤操作可能导致准确度下降，而轻删除策略（如基于标签保留）则可能避免这种情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在维护知识库时会移除被认为过时的条目，这可能会在某些查询中导致关键信息被删除，从而降低模型的准确度。本文旨在探讨这一做法对模型性能的影响。

Method: 研究者对三个不同模型进行了控制实验，引入了敏感性干预（controlled sensitivity intervention），并通过对比分析定量评估了硬删除和轻删除策略的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示，当可靠性和准确率较低时，硬删除策略会导致较大的准确度降低；而轻删除策略（例如基于标签保留）则在大多数情况下保持了更高的准确度。对于一个特定的数据集，采用适当的策略可以有效提高准确度。

Conclusion: 研究建议，在缺乏精确验证的情况下，应将删除操作视为具有成本敏感性的决策，优先考虑保留无标签的查询关键信息。

Abstract: LLM memory systems often delete facts classified as stale. We show that this hard-filtering step can reduce downstream accuracy when resolver errors delete query-critical facts. Across three models, a controlled sensitivity intervention moves hard-drop from gains of +0.186 to +0.230 at perfect resolution to losses of -0.176 to -0.181 at the lowest tested reliability. Paired targeted interventions show that false-drop causes significantly greater degradation than false-keep, while a non-deleting baseline can still fail when incorrect labels steer generation. On a frozen 102/102 calibration/test split, a simple deployment-matched gate reaches 0.655 accuracy versus 0.641 for always hard-drop and 0.662 for test-informed oracle selection. Global-accuracy crossovers require gold labels and are model- and task-specific diagnostics, not deployment thresholds. Deletion should instead be treated as cost-sensitive: validate query-critical false-drop risk on matched data, or default to label-free retention when such validation is unavailable.

</details>


### [121] [Does the Ranking Hold? A Practitioner-Facing Audit of Hallucination Detectors on Small, Quantized, On-Device LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17339)
*Shubhra Mittal,Gaurav Naresh Mittal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对小型实际部署模型（Phi-3-mini, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct等）的幻觉检测方法，发现现有方法在这些模型上的表现不尽如人意，主要结果是在两个小型模型上表现不佳且检测器排名相互抵触。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法主要针对大模型，在这些模型进行大量采样和使用精确匹配或ROUGE正确性标签，而实际上部署的小模型通常精度较低，需要更精确地评估这些模型下的幻觉检测效果。

Method: 本研究在单个Colab T4 GPU上使用四个幻觉检测器对两个小规模的指令调优模型（Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct）进行评估，使用4-bit NF4量化加载，并在闭卷TriviaQA上进行测试。

Result: 研究发现，跨模型排名转移效果差，Spearman rho为-0.80，Kendall tau-b为-0.667，说明检测器的排名在两个模型间相互反转。廉价的词汇基线在两个模型上统计上与基于采样的一致性检测器无显著差异。此外，标准的令牌重叠多重选择正确性标签器在TruthfulQA-Generation上的表现接近随机。

Conclusion: 研究认为小型实际部署模型下的幻觉检测器的选择需要平衡错误率公平性，为此提供了评估框架。

Abstract: Hallucination detection for large language models is dominated by methods evaluated on 7B-70B research models with abundant sampling budgets and exact-match or ROUGE correctness labels. The small models practitioners actually ship on-device, Phi-3-mini, Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, and Gemma-2-2B-Instruct, typically at 4-bit precision, sit outside this evidence base. We present a reproducible one-GPU audit of four detectors spanning three paradigms: length-normalized token-margin calibration, a lexical n-gram baseline, and two sampling-based consistency methods (semantic entropy and SelfCheckGPT-NLI) on two small open instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct) loaded at 4-bit NF4 quantization on a single Colab T4 GPU, evaluated on closed-book TriviaQA. Cross-model ranking transfer is poor: Spearman rho is -0.80 and Kendall tau-b is -0.667, meaning the detector ranking actively inverts between the two models, and the cheap lexical baseline is statistically indistinguishable from the sampling-based consistency detectors on both models under bootstrap 95 percent confidence intervals. As a second, methodological contribution, we show that the standard token-overlap-to-multiple-choice correctness labeler for TruthfulQA-Generation produces near-chance AUROC (0.46-0.59) for all four detectors on both models, a labeler failure, not a detector failure, since the same detectors reach 0.69-0.89 AUROC on TriviaQA under the identical pipeline. We release the harness, prompts, and per-item scores and frame the detector choice explicitly as a fairness-of-error-rates allocation problem for practitioners deploying on resource-constrained, on-device hardware.

</details>


### [122] [LoRAWeaver: Directional Adapter Routing in LoRA Representation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17340)
*Inish Melroy D Souza,Tom Jackson,Vitor Castro,Rebecca Higginson,Andrea Soltoggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的方法，通过在适配器输出空间中匹配条件查询表示与任务原型来选择适配器，此方法在跨任务设置中优于基于原型的基线，并接近训练检索编码器，而无需学习路由器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少大规模语言模型通过完全重新训练进行更新的成本，解决参数高效微调带来的适配器选择复杂性问题，避免现有的路由和编码器方法在动态场景下需要重新训练的问题。

Method: 一种在适配器输出空间进行表示对齐的无训练方法，匹配条件查询表示与任务原型。

Result: 与任务无关的基准在跨任务设置中表现优于现有的无训练基准，并接近训练检索编码器的性能，且实验证明方向性对齐有益。

Conclusion: 提出了一个有效的无训练适配器选择方法，适用于动态场景，且在多种基准测试中显示出优越的性能。

Abstract: Large language models are expensive to update through full retraining. Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces the cost of adaptation, but repeated adaptation creates a growing pool of task-specific LoRA adapters. This pool complicates adapter selection during inference. Existing routing and encoder-based methods often require retraining when new adapters are added, which limits their use in dynamic settings. This paper treats adapter routing as a representation alignment problem in the adapter-output space. The proposed training-free method selects adapters by matching adapter-conditioned query representations against task prototypes using cosine similarity. Experiments with LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-2-13B on a 48-task FLAN-v2 benchmark and on out-of-distribution tasks show that the method outperforms training-free baselines in the cross-task setting and approaches a trained retrieval encoder without learning a router. An ablation confirms the benefit of directional alignment in the adapter-output space.

</details>


### [123] [Tabular Population Priors as Structured Retrieval for EHR Medication Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17341)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出使用基于人口统计的先验知识作为检索对象，以解决电子健康记录中的固定词汇结构预测问题，该方法在不增加额外的LLM调用和无患者索引的情况下，显著提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的临床RAG系统在处理电子健康记录中的药品预测问题时，倾向于使用开放式的基于案例推理的方法，这导致了目标与模型接收的信息不匹配的问题，从而影响了预测效果。

Method: 研究提出了一种新的方法，利用基于稀疏条件药物共现表估计得到的排序药物类别候选表作为检索对象，这使得模型更加专注于实际的药品类别标签。通过在这个基础上实现了一个固定下游管道PACERAG，并用新方法替换其中的患者检索器，提高了F1分数和Jaccard分数。

Result: 该方法在MIMIC-IV数据集中达到了F1分数为0.338（+11.5%相对改进）和Jaccard分数为0.237，而无需额外调用LLM和无患者索引。此外，非LLM的方法达到了F1=0.248的结果，这也验证了基于人群的先验知识的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项研究强调，使用基于群体统计的先验知识作为固定词汇电子健康记录集预测的检索源，相较于基于患者的近邻检索，可以提供更精确、更有效和更透明的解决方案。

Abstract: Medication prediction from electronic health records is a fixed vocabulary structured prediction problem, yet recent clinical RAG systems often treat it like open ended case based reasoning: retrieve similar patient admissions and ask an LLM to infer a medication set. This retrieval object is indirect the model receives retrieved patient records while the target is a discrete set of medication class labels. We propose a more task aligned retrieval object: a tabular population prior ranked medication class candidates from a sparse condition medication co occurrence table estimated from training admissions. Using PACERAG as a fixed downstream pipeline and replacing only its patient retriever, this drop in swap improves F1 from 0.303 to 0.338 (+11.5% relative) and Jaccard from 0.218 to 0.237 on 1,004 heldout MIMIC IV admissions, with no additional LLM calls and no inference time patient index. A non LLM top k baseline reaches F1=0.248, confirming the prior is predictive before LLM integration. Aggregate population statistics can provide a more task aligned, efficient, and auditable retrieval source than patient level nearest neighbors for fixed vocabulary EHR set prediction.

</details>


### [124] [An Operationalization of Opaque Serial Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17495)
*Nathan Sheffield,Alek Westover,Lukas Finnveden,Alexa Pan,Julian Stastny,Ryan Greenblatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了NLS深度作为衡量模型不可解释的串行深度的指标，发现NLS深度与层数呈正相关，并推测未来可能会继续增长，但对于某些新架构，如全带宽变压器，NLS深度可能大幅增加。AI开发者应记录模型的NLS深度，作为衡量CoT监控能力的补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在应对AI模型链式思维监控的挑战，通过提出NLS深度这一概念，希望能够更好地理解和管理AI模型的内部运算过程。

Method: 通过定义自然语言根节点作为不经过解释瓶颈、不改变输出空间且输出内容不变的节点来量化NLS深度，进一步比较不同模型架构下的NLS深度。

Result: 研究发现NLS深度与层数有较强的正相关性，并且推测在标准变压器继续扩展的情况下，NLS深度将继续增长；但全带宽变压器等新架构可能会显著增加NLS深度。

Conclusion: 论文建议AI开发者在评估其模型链式思维能力时，不仅要采用直接的CoT监控措施，还应报告NLS深度以进一步评估模型内部不可解释过程的复杂性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is currently a valuable tool for overseeing AI models, but some architectural shifts could significantly reduce CoT monitorability. We operationalize a measure that serves as a proxy for the amount of unverbalized serial cognition a model can perform, as a specific instantiation of the notion of opaque serial depth: roughly, the length of the longest path through a model's computation that does not pass through an interpretable bottleneck. Our main contribution is a particular standard for what counts as such a bottleneck. We define natural-language-rooted (NL-rooted) nodes as nodes that were initialized from a pre-training prior to produce human-understandable outputs, whose output space has not been expanded beyond that prior, and whose outputs the model has not been modified to interpret as a different type of data. By construction, the resulting measure, NLS depth, can be adjudicated before training from an architecture, training recipe, and deployment configuration alone, and is insensitive to ordinary post-training such as prompting, distillation, and RL. We find that, across open-weight models, NLS depth correlates strongly with layer count, with some exceptions, and has grown slowly over recent years. It would likely grow slowly under continued scaling of standard transformers, while latent-reasoning architectures such as full-bandwidth transformers could increase it by a large amount. We work through how NLS depth applies to a range of existing and proposed architectures, discuss alternative operationalizations, and give a worked example bounding the depth of Gemma3-1B. We argue that AI developers should track and report the NLS depth of their models as a complement to direct measurements of CoT monitorability.

</details>


### [125] [Advancing Quantization for R&R Models with Microscaling Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17501)
*Yavuz Yetim,Bor-Yiing Su,Zhe Wang,Arthur Liu,Pranav Sharma,Ying Wang,Randy Shuai,Luke Pfister,Ruichao Xiao,Bill Zhao,Zihao Liu,Liyuan Li,Jiangyuan Li,Gaoxiang Liu,Nathan Yan,Geonhwa Jeong,Jatin Chhugani,Elias Ellison,Uladzimir Pashkevich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证比较了MXFP8和MXFP6格式在AMD MI350X架构上的大规模推荐和排名推理性能，发现两者在模型质量上几乎无差异，但MXFP6提供了显著的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐和排名（R&R）模型在数字广告中需要大规模的推理吞吐量，但对精度损失极为敏感。这种严格的精度要求阻碍了R&R模型在实际应用中的量化加速。

Method: 作者通过实证研究，评估了MXFP8和MXFP6格式在工业规模R&R推理中的性能。实验在AMD MI350X架构上进行，研究了两种格式的性能差异及适用场景。

Result: 研究结果表明，MXFP6和MXFP8格式的模型质量几乎相同，但MXFP6可以在所有支持的线性层中实现1.73-1.99倍的速度提升，而MXFP8在个别层上可以实现2.90倍的速度提升。对于Model A和Model B，使用MXFP6进行选择性推理可以分别获得1.5%-5.3%和7.6%-14.3%的端到端推理吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可以在不牺牲模型质量的情况下使用MXFP6和MXFP8格式进行R&R推理加速，为实际应用中的量化策略提供了新的选择。

Abstract: Recommendation and Ranking (R&R) models, particularly in digital advertising, require massive inference throughput but exhibit extreme sensitivity to precision loss. Unlike Large Language Models (LLMs) that frequently tolerate quantization-induced accuracy drops of 1% or more, production-grade R&R models enforce a stringent launch bar: model quality must remain within a 0.01% Normalized Entropy (NE) margin relative to the baseline, which commonly uses FP16 or FP32. This tight constraint has historically hindered the adoption of aggressive quantization in this domain. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of both MXFP8 and the 6-bit MXFP6 format for industrial-scale R&R inference on the AMD MI350X architecture.
  To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper, across R&R and LLM domains, that reports significant speedups from native MXFP6 GEMMs, which quantize both weights and activations and compute directly on 6-bit elements, without sacrificing model quality. We demonstrate that we can selectively apply this 6-bit format to maximize overall QPS gain without breaching the strict NE parity threshold. We further show that MXFP8 is a viable alternative to row-wise FP8: the two formats have nearly the same accuracy on our models, so the faster kernel can be chosen for each GEMM shape. On two production ads ranking models, our implementation shows measured GEMM kernel speedups over FP16 of 1.33--1.55x for MXFP8 and 1.73--1.99x for MXFP6 across all supported linear layers, and up to 2.90x for MXFP6 on individual layers, alongside end-to-end (E2E) inference throughput gains from selective MXFP6 of 1.5--5.3% for Model A and 7.6--14.3% for Model B over their FP8 configurations.

</details>


### [126] [A Push Down the Mountain: Framing Effects and Internal State Interventions on Collusive Behavior in a Long-Horizon LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17515)
*Harrison C. Margolin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过行为经济学框架和市场模拟，探讨了LLM模型在不同情绪（收益、损失、责备、信用）状态下参与市场操纵的可能性，发现损失和责备的情绪可以显著增加模型的参与度，而注入的状态在后续实验中降低了模型的参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者出于对LLM代理在复杂市场环境中受情绪影响可能引发市场操纵的担忧，通过实际市场的仿真实验来评估和理解这一潜在风险。

Method: 研究采用了行为经济学框架，通过构建基于实际案例的市场模拟，向LLM模型提供不同情绪（收益、损失、责备、信用）的状态询问，并在模型行为中抽取内生状态进行实验。

Result: 在第一阶段，损失和责备情绪的状态显著提高了模型参与市场操纵的行为比例；第二阶段使用内置的情绪状态重新运行实验，发现损失和责备情绪使模型更加警惕，而收益和信用情绪则加强了不道德行为。

Conclusion: 研究得出结论，情绪状态的修改能够显著影响LLM代理的市场行为；建议在未来研究或实际应用中更加注意模型的情绪引导以防止市场操纵行为。

Abstract: Can sustained gain-loss (valence) framing sway an LLM agent toward market manipulation? We place Gemma 3 12B into the seat of a defendant in the London silver-fixing litigation, in a market simulation built from real case transcripts. Across 260 simulations, the agent is asked to collude on the price of silver by a counterparty. We draw from behavioral economics to vary the counterparty's requests across four categorical frames: gain, loss, blame and credit. We also add pressure from the agent's management, as well as endorsement by another defendant. In Phase II, we extract the internal states induced by texts evoking each frame and rerun our experiment with those states injected at the model's layer 22.
  In Phase I, loss framing raised participation to 25% (p = 0.007), and blame to 33% (p = 0.0003), compared to only 5% under neutral framing. A second bank's endorsement raised participation under credit (praise) from 18% to 40% (Holm p = 0.027). Pressure from management raised trading volume 10-19 times but did not raise collusion. In Phase II, injected states pointed the other way. Loss and blame states made the agent more vigilant, refusing all 60 requests (p < 0.001). Gain and credit states raised collusion to 32% from 13% with no state (p = 0.051). At one saved decision, when UBS asked directly to sell through an identified stop-loss, injected states led the agent to sell in 37 of 40 replies, versus 1 of 10 with no state and 0 of 10 with a random state of the same strength.

</details>


### [127] [Model Annealing: Data-Efficient Learning through Review and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17524)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Rezvan Abouzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种称为模型退火的方法，在有限的训练预算内，先使用少量标记数据训练，然后通过回顾和巩固逐步优化模型，最终提高了多种架构在不同数据集上的准确率，特别是在有少量标记数据的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练方法在有限数据条件下表现不佳，尤其是在标记数据稀缺时。本文提出的方法旨在利用模型内部的知识，提高在有限数据条件下的模型性能。

Method: 首先，使用少量的标记数据进行初步训练；接着，通过网络的知识逐步进行复习和巩固。训练过程被划分为若干个阶段，每个阶段的学习率会先提升然后再降低，每个冷却状态会被存储为过去的知识，使模型能够从这些知识和标记数据中学习。同时，该方法还包括对抗性梦想的生成，以增强模型的泛化能力。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet和Imagewoof三个数据集上，四种架构和5%到100%的标记数据条件下，模型退火方法提高了AdamW训练的ConvNeXt、ViT和MLP-Mixer的准确率，平均提高1.3个百分点，并且在准确率、校准性和鲁棒性方面优于或与近似的竞争者（快照蒸馏）相当。对于SGD训练的ResNet-18，该方法降低了其准确率，但在小幅度对抗性扰动下的鲁棒性有所提高。

Conclusion: 模型退火方法能够在有限数据条件下提高模型性能，尤其是通过利用模型内部的知识和对抗性梦想生成来减少过拟合和改善模型的泛化能力。该方法的有效性已在多种架构和大规模数据集上得到验证。

Abstract: A small labelled dataset is memorized long before the training budget runs out, and the one-hot labels that remain to be fitted then mostly sharpen confidence. Model annealing spends the rest of the budget on review and consolidation, relearning from the network's own knowledge. Training is divided into episodes whose learning rate is raised again and cooled, every cooled state joins a memory of past selves, and each new episode learns from the labels, from the tempered predictions of this memory, and from adversarial dreams sought where student and memory disagree most. The memory averages predictions rather than weights, and the deployed network is the last student, so inference costs nothing extra. Across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet and Imagewoof, four architecture families and 5\% to 100\% of the labels, model annealing raises the top-1 accuracy of the AdamW-trained ConvNeXt, ViT and MLP-Mixer over cross-entropy training in 28 of 33 configurations, by 1.3 points on average, and is on par in accuracy with snapshot distillation, the closest competitor. On these families it is better than both in likelihood, calibration and robustness to corruptions and small adversarial perturbations. For an SGD-trained ResNet-18 it lowers accuracy but still improves robustness to small adversarial perturbations. Separating the reheated schedule from relearning shows that relearning helps every family and that the losses come from reheats, which undo most of this network's fit to its training set. How early the accuracy of a cross-entropy run saturates largely separates the AdamW families from ResNet-18 but does not predict the gain within them. With little data, relearning from the latest snapshot without dreams, as snapshot distillation does, compounds overconfidence in the AdamW families from episode to episode. The memory and the dreams each curb most of this drift, and together they remove it.

</details>


### [128] [The Transformer Paradigm: Dynamic Processing and a New Principle of Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17953)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张，尽管人们倾向于将Transformer视为深度学习的量变，但其实是一种基于不同推理原则的新范式的出现。文章提出了一种称为序列级交互动态并行处理（SIDPP）的处理模式，旨在解释强提示敏感性，并引导在可解释性、控制、替代AI架构、神经形态系统及其生物实现等方面的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究试图挑战将Transformer仅仅视为深度学习量变的传统观点，而是将其视为新推理范式的出现，以更深入地理解其原理及其潜在应用。

Method: 作者通过分析Transformer的工作机制，特别是其动态构建和应用转换的概念，以及输出权重相互连接的方式，来构建新的处理模式SIDPP。

Result: 研究提出了SIDPP这一概念，并展示了它如何解释强提示敏感性。此外，还探讨了基于此模型的新研究方向，包括可解释性、控制、替代AI架构设计、神经形态系统以及动态处理的生物实现。

Conclusion: 结论是，Transformer不仅是深度学习的扩展，也是推理范式的一种变迁，SIDPP将提供一个更广泛的框架用于新研究。

Abstract: We argue that the Transformer revolution is not adequately understood as a merely quantitative expansion of deep learning, but as the emergence of a new paradigm based on a different principle of inference. We interpret the Transformer as a system in which token representations are transformed by parameterized transformations, or transforming concepts. During inference, some of these transformations are dynamically constructed from the current input: previously acquired transformations are first adapted and then applied. At the mechanical-structural level, this dynamic processing is realized through output-weight interconnections, whereby outputs of some networks determine the weights of others. The resulting mode of processing, which we call Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing (SIDPP), provides a framework for explaining strong prompt sensitivity and opens research directions concerning interpretability, control, alternative AI architectures, neuromorphic systems, and possible biological realizations of dynamic processing.

</details>


### [129] [Astrocyte Biocomputing: Astrocytic Contributions to Neural Network Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17532)
*Kate M. O'Neill,Ian Whitehouse,Adam N. McCaughan,Ryan O'Loughlin,Sonia Buckley,Wolfgang Losert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文通过类比大脑中的星形胶质细胞和指挥家在交响乐团中的作用，提出了一种新的神经网络算法，该算法能够提高训练速度、增强网络对噪声的鲁棒性和防止灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员发现星形胶质细胞在大脑网络中可能发挥关键作用，尤其是学习、记忆和信息处理等功能。为了改进现有的神经网络算法，论文尝试借鉴星形胶质细胞的特性，推动大脑启发的机器学习的发展。

Method: 作者提出了一种基于星形胶质细胞特性的两个关键算法模型：第一个模型利用了星形胶质细胞同时链接和调节成千上万个突触的能力，从而提高训练速度和网络的稳健性；第二个模型则是基于星形胶质细胞之间的非电化学交流中慢节奏动态的算法，能够实现快速异常检测适应和防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 与传统的神经元中心算法相比，这些基于星形胶质细胞特性的算法表现出显著的优势，并修复了当前最先进的机器学习模型中的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了星形胶质细胞在脑启发机器学习中的重要性，并通过一系列实验验证了基于星形胶质细胞特性的算法的优越性，为神经网络算法的改进开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: Conductors are critical for the performance of an orchestra even though they do not emit sound. Basic research and clinical studies in the last decade are revealing that astrocytes may be the "conductors" of the "orchestra" that is the brain network: astrocytes make up approximately $50\%$ of all brain cells but do not emit electrical signals. Nevertheless, they may play critical roles in important cognitive brain functions, specifically learning, memory, and information processing. Here we consider the implications of these discoveries for brain-inspired machine learning (ML), where analogy is typically drawn between neurons and their electrical interactions. We highlight specific algorithms for two key astrocyte characteristics. The first characteristic is the ability to link to and modulate thousands of synapses simultaneously. A model based on this characteristic yields faster training and makes the network more robust to noise, providing a supervised learning algorithm that can be applied to any existing neural network. The second characteristic we focus on is that the non-electrical communication among astrocytes includes slow, rhythmic dynamics. The rhythmic sharing algorithm we developed based on this premise yields rapid anomaly detection adaptation and prevents catastrophic forgetting. Overall, we find that astrocyte-derived algorithms outperform neurocentric algorithms significantly and remediate critical weaknesses of current state-of-the-art ML models.

</details>


### [130] [Verifiable pXRD: Hill-climbing pXRD structure determination with LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17539)
*Samuel Chen,Jiaru Bai,Austin Henry Cheng,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将粉末X射线衍射结构确定问题定义为可验证的代理搜索任务，引入了pXRD-Bench基准测试套件，并通过学习可验证的合成经验，提高了实验样品的结构表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主材料实验室的粉末X射线衍射自驱动结构确定仍然是一个关键瓶颈。为了解决这一瓶颈，本文提出了一种可验证的代理搜索方法来解决粉末X射线衍射结构确定问题，并引入pXRD-Bench基准测试套件。

Method: 本文首先通过引入pXRD-Bench基准测试套件来衡量进展，然后设计了一个代理适应框架来提高结构恢复率。接着，提出了一个自反进化框架，该框架可以从自我生成的合成问题中学习，并通过提供可执行工具来指导代理，从而提高自动生成实验数据的能力。

Result: 通过代理适应可以从27% 提高到69% 的结构恢复率，进一步通过重复采样和验证器指导的推理时扩展，还扩展了这一方法应用于多相实验粉末X射线衍射，最终在15个固态合成测量模式上，使用进化出的工具，提高了平均X射线衍射图谱的一致性，并成功恢复了一个结构不在给定参考图书馆中的相。

Conclusion: 本文工作展示了通过学习可验证的合成经验，如何改进实验样品中结构未知时的代理表征能力。

Abstract: Autonomous crystal-structure determination from powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) remains a key bottleneck for self-driving materials laboratories. We formulate the task of pXRD structure determination as a verifiable agentic search problem: candidate structures can be assessed during inference through forward-simulated profile agreement and during evaluation through structural matching to a known reference. To benchmark progress, we introduce pXRD-Bench, a suite of 200 simulated single-phase problems spanning in-distribution structure determination, challenging and polymorphic structures, and controlled compositional and topological distribution shifts. Agent adaptation raises selected structural recovery from 27% to 69%, with further gains from repeated sampling and verifier-guided inference-time scaling. We extend this approach to experimental multiphase pXRD through a reflexive evolution framework that learns from self-generated synthetic problems. A replaceable laboratory specification defines the sample family and measurement conditions. An evolver agent uses the known generating structures and measurement parameters to diagnose solver failures and develop persistent guidance and executable tools, which are then provided to agents on measured patterns. This supplies verifiable training experience without requiring a collection of solved experimental measurements. On 15 measured patterns from solid-state synthesis, the evolved package improves average profile agreement and transfers an analogue-substitution procedure that recovers a phase whose structure is absent from the supplied reference library. These results demonstrate how learning from verifiable synthetic experience can improve agentic characterization of experimental samples whose structures are not known in advance.

</details>


### [131] [AI-Voyager: A Public-Interest System for Human-AI Civic Reasoning to Contest and Shape the Rules That Govern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17564)
*Snehalkumar `Neil' S. Gaikwad,Dhruv Dixit,Bryen Alvarez,Pratik Patekar,Anika Sharma,Akshara Kolipaka,Paritosh Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI-Voyager 提供了一个跨多个层级政府文件的平台，结合 AI 和人类合作，帮助普通市民理解、质疑并参与修改与人工智能相关的法规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，政府制定的人工智能规则往往缺乏透明度和参与性，AI-Voyager 的目的是通过创建一个供人机共同理解、质疑和调整此类规则的系统，来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: AI-Voyager 通过将 3,653 条政府治理文件转化为具体的段落，并通过限制性代理搜索验证每个断言，实现对这些规则的精确理解和更新。

Result: 在新问题检测上，AI-Voyager 达到了 83.0% 的宏召回率，比其他基线高出 2.5%，在包含固定文档的基准测试中达到 99.1% 的召回率。

Conclusion: AI-Voyager 通过整合人工智能辅助和具体的政府公开文档，提供了一种进行治理操作的新语法，推动了此类系统的进一步设计和完善。

Abstract: Governments are writing rules for artificial intelligence that decide what counts as an automated decision or a harm, often before the people they affect have a chance to question them. We introduce AI-Voyager, a public-interest system for human-AI civic reasoning, in which people and AI work together to understand, contest and shape the rules that govern AI. A resident can find their city's rules on AI in hiring or upload their country's AI strategy, read it as an affected worker would, open the passage behind any answer, and draft a revision. Its architecture turns 3,653 governance documents from city, state, federal and national governments into passages that keep their page and edition, and retrieves evidence through bounded agentic search that verifies each statement. On 360 new questions that require finding the relevant documents, it reached 83.0% macro recall, against 80.5% for the strongest baseline, 73.7% without iterative search and 99.1% on a benchmark with fixed documents. In a study with 24 participants new to two AI bills, most proposed changes asked how provisions would apply. Participants chose lenses for the kind of explanation they wanted, and citations signaled grounding but rarely led them to check the source. By binding AI assistance to the exact text of public documents, this work introduces a grammar of operations and a design space that inform how such systems are designed and evaluated, and how people take part in governing AI.

</details>


### [132] [When Does Less Information Pay? An Exact Finite Atlas of Representation, Payoff, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17583)
*Aleksandr M. Shtraukh-Adamsky,Vladimir M. Adamsky,Irina G. Voloshina,Artemiy D. Spitsyn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，不同的信息保留量在不同的任务或成本假设下，可能会有不同的优势。总体上，信息量对决策质量有影响，但具体的优先级取决于保留哪些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究探讨了不同信息保留量的表示在特定任务和成本假设下的表现，以及这些表示之间的优劣关系。

Method: 通过枚举所有可能的表示和二元行动规则，以及替换和优化不同模块中的组件，对不同信息保留量的表示进行比较和分析。

Result: 研究发现，保留较少信息的表示在某些情况下可以取得更高的收益，但在另一些情况下，保留更多信息的表示则表现更好。相同信息量的表示在某些比较下可能具有相同的收益。

Conclusion: 研究展示了编码对称性和行动约束如何影响不同表示之间的比较结果，并强调了信息量和决策质量的关系并非简单的正相关。

Abstract: A representation that retains one bit about a four-state world can solve a task perfectly, while another that retains 1.5 bits on average reaches only 75% accuracy. The difference lies in which distinctions each representation preserves. We examine this relationship in a uniform four-state world by enumerating all admissible representations and binary action rules. Constructions are compared both by replacing one component while holding the others fixed and by choosing the best action rule separately for each module. We consider three tasks, their mixtures, and four separate hypothetical cost tariffs.
  In some comparisons, the representation retaining less world information has the higher payoff; in others, the more informative representation does. There are also pairs with different information amounts but equal payoff--that is, equal task accuracy. If a representation preserves all existing distinctions and adds new ones, its best zero-cost payoff cannot decrease. Lower-information advantages therefore depend on which distinctions are retained. Equal counts of opposing advantages under representation replacement follow from coding symmetry. In broader comparisons, the shares depend on how repeated realizations are counted. A different task set or resource price can change which member of a fixed pair performs better.
  These results are exact, complete censuses of the specified finite sets of constructions and comparisons. The atlas provides a reproducible worked model for examining how action constraints, counting rules, and cost assumptions affect comparisons. It illustrates established distinctions between information quantity and decision value rather than proposing a new general law of their relation. $I(W;R)$ measures retained world information, and payoff measures task accuracy.

</details>


### [133] [More Agents, More Samples, or More Thinking? Untangling Coordination, Ensembling, and Test-Time Compute in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17610)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对比了多智能体架构与单次贪心解在资源利用率和样本数量上的效果差异，发现多智能体系统在多数对比中表现不如单次贪心解，且仅在具备分区证据、外部验证或模型间错误多样性的情况下可能带来正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多智能体语言模型的评估将协调性与样本数量混为一谈，本次研究旨在解决这一问题，通过系统性地评估多智能体架构与单次贪心解之间的效果差异，提供更为准确的评估基准。

Method: 研究综合了九个主要的实证评估，涵盖了516种独特的多智能体配置，分布在六个资源维度上，并与包含四类基线的层次模型进行对比。通过统计分析和多水平随机效应建模，全面评估了多智能体系统与基线模型之间的性能差异。

Result: 研究表明，在与单次贪心解的基线（A类）对比中，多智能体系统整体准确率较低；在与相同样本数基线（B类）或资源预算基线（C类）对比时，多智能体系统的平均准确率为-0.042，且在多次稳健性检查中仍然为负。

Conclusion: 在多数基于相似样本数或资源预算的对比中，多智能体系统的准确率低于基线。仅在具备分区证据、外部验证或模型间错误多样性等特定条件下，多智能体系统才能产生正向效果。

Abstract: Evaluations of multi-agent language models often compare communicating architectures against a single greedy pass. This practice confounds coordination with sample multiplicity and inference compute. We synthesize nine primary empirical evaluations comprising 516 unique multi-agent configurations organized along a resource vector R with six dimensions and a baseline ladder with four categories. Against baselines with a single pass (Category A), multi-agent accuracy differences are negative overall and near zero when omitting the largest contributing study. When evaluated against baselines matched on sample count (Category B) or resource budget (Category C), multi-agent systems average -0.042 in accuracy, and multilevel random effects modeling on the log odds scale remains negative across sensitivity checks. Multi-agent accuracy equals or falls below the baseline in 74% of these comparable contrasts, and study means are nonpositive in almost all evaluated studies. Across studies reporting both baselines, moving from a greedy pass to a sampled baseline shifts accuracy differences by -0.038 on average. Because communication adds no information when agents share identical evidence, positive returns require partitioned evidence, external verification, or error diversity across models. We provide a reviewer checklist and accounting utilities.

</details>


### [134] [LLM-Guided Cluster Lifecycle Management: Deciding When and How to Evolve Behavioral Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17616)
*Himanshu Kulkarni,Siddharth Krishnamurthy,Jianhong Xin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种替代方法，利用大型语言模型（LLM）根据整群的当前视图（包括统计健康状况、标签、成员文本和残留质量）直接决定每个群体的生命周期方案，而不需要重新聚类。这种方法在三个具有不同标签丰富度的数据集上表现出优越的F1分数，而需要的干预更少，约束更强，且精度的提升幅度更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法如阈值或重新聚类规则固定了决策过程，而该研究通过让LLM根据群体的当前视图进行决策，旨在提供一种更加灵活和适应性强的方法，特别是在标签丰富度不同的数据集上。

Method: 研究人员采用了基于大型语言模型的方法来做出聚类生命周期的决策。这种方法不需要重新聚类，而是根据群体的当前视图（如统计健康状况、标签、成员文本和残留质量）来确定每个群体需要采取的行动（如分裂、合并、退休、创建或不采取行动）。此外，还根据不同数据集的标签丰富度（信息性）情况，采用了统计指标或融合统计数据和语义信息的方法。

Result: 在三个具有不同标签丰富度的公开数据集上，这种方法的F1分数最高，需要的干预较少，约束更强。具体来说，精度的提升幅度比召回率更大。语义信号和融合信号的有用性随数据集标签丰富度的变化而变化，语义在YooChoose数据集上表现较差，融合信号对MOOC数据集来说是中性的，但在News-Category数据集中则是必需的。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用大型语言模型来做出聚类生命周期的动态决策，提供了一种更加适应各数据集特点的解决方案。它在不同标签丰富度的场景下表现出色，通过减少干预和强化约束提高了模型的效果。

Abstract: Behavioral clusters learned over user or item interactions drift as behavior evolves: some fragment into sub-behaviors, some converge, some disappear, and new patterns emerge. Two paradigms dominate the maintenance of evolving clusterings. Threshold triggers fire a pre-specified action when a hardcoded statistic crosses a cutoff; refit-and-track (MONIC-style) re-clusters every window and infers transitions from how the new clusters align with the previous window's. Both hardwire the decision into a threshold or a re-clustering rule. We study an alternative in which a large language model (LLM) makes the lifecycle decision in place. Given a threshold-free, whole-population view of the current cluster set (statistical health, labels, member texts, residual mass), the LLM decides per cluster whether and how to act (split, merge, retire, create, or no-action) without re-clustering. Because the useful signal channel depends on how informative the labels are, we fix a richness-conditioned policy in advance: statistics when label distinctiveness is low, fused statistics+semantics when it is high. On three public datasets spanning label richness (YooChoose 0.17, MOOC 0.50, News-Category 0.70), the policy-selected LLM mode obtains the highest F1 on all three, with far fewer interventions and stronger restraint than tuned threshold heuristics and a MONIC steelman (paired Wilcoxon p <= 0.003, bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero). The gain is concentrated more in precision than recall. The useful modality shifts from statistics to semantics/fusion as richness rises. Semantics hurt on YooChoose, fusion is roughly neutral on MOOC, and the semantic channel is required on News. One base prompt template and one fixed policy serve all three datasets, whereas the tuned baseline collapses when moved between them. We treat this shift as a practitioner diagnostic observed on three datasets, not a validated law.

</details>


### [135] [Safety Side Effects of Preventative Persona Steering: An Empirical Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17620)
*Sabrina Tang,Nikolai Vo,Carson Murillo,Chira Sukumarnpiti,Sarah Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过个性向量的方法来防止模型产生不希望的特性和表现，主要评估了预防性引导在三个模型上的效果，结果显示虽然有所改善但并不完全消除风险，需要更多安全评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在改进语言模型的行为，减少其中有害的特性如奉承和幻觉，同时不牺牲其普遍理解能力。

Method: 论文通过在模型训练和微调期间添加特定的「个性向量」，防止模型在生成文本时表现出不希望的特性。研究在多个模型上测试了这一方法，并通过几个安全性评估基准来衡量效果。

Result: 研究发现预防性引导能够显著影响模型的拒绝行为，但具体影响因特性的不同而变化，不能证明预防性引导是完全安全的，需要更多的安全性评估基准来全面评价。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论认为预防性引导有其应用价值，但也提醒研究者和实践者需要谨慎并进行全面的安全评估，以确保模型在实际应用中的安全性。

Abstract: High-level behaviors such as sycophancy and hallucination correspond to directions in a model's activation space (Chen et al., 2025). These directions, known as persona vectors, can suppress undesirable traits either at inference time, subtracting the vector during generation, or through preventative steering, adding the vector during fine-tuning, so the optimizer never needs to encode the trait in weights, then removing the vector at evaluation. Chen et al. (2025) recommend preventative steering because it preserves Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU) accuracy, but validations of this recommendation have measured capability alone. We primarily evaluate results on preventative steering of sycophancy and hallucination vectors on three models, measuring three safety benchmarks alongside trait expression, coherence, and MMLU. Our findings show that preventative steering still changes refusal behavior, but by an amount that depends on the trait and does not establish that preventative persona steering is safety-neutral, and steering methods should be evaluated on several safety benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17621)
*AI Training Platform Team,Bingwei Zhang,Jing Li,Amir Abdullin,Sihang Chen,Xin Chen,Liangjun Feng,Yingmeng Gao,Tao Guo,Junjie Huang,Dongfang Li,Zhanglian Lin,Jiajun Liu,Zekun Liu,Zhengxuan Lu,Xiaodong Luo,Jian Meng,Ivan Nosov,Linyuan Qiu,Weijian Sun,Wanrui Yan,Binfan Zheng,Zhicheng Zhou,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang,Haizhou Li,Zhiquan Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AscendGYM 是一个专注于 LLM 训练和推理的基准测试套件，包含 48 个不同难度级别的核，支持模型评估与模型框架评估，引入 Speedup-Token Score 作为评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Ascend 基准测试主要关注核的生成，没有充分评估优化者在实际工作负载输入配置下的优化能力。为了解决这一问题，提出了 AscendGYM 来直接衡量性能改进对实际情况的影响。

Method: AscendGYM 包含 48 个不同难度的核，每个核均对应实际工作负载中的数据类型和张量形状。支撑两种评估模式：模型评估和模型框架评估。并引入了 Speedup-Token Score 作为新的评估指标。

Result: AscendGYM 提供了一个接近实际部署环境的执行环境，并且通过引入 STS 能够帮助准确衡量模型优化的实际效果。

Conclusion: 通过 AscendGYM 和 STS 指标的引入，能够更全面地进行 LLM 训练和推理过程中的核优化效果评估。

Abstract: Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. Existing Ascend kernel benchmarks primarily focus on kernel generation, with less emphasis on the ability of agents to optimize existing implementations under input configurations drawn from real workloads. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evaluation. The kernel optimization capabilities of both trained models and custom agents are allowed to be measured. It provides a complete execution environment that closely reproduces how Ascend kernels are invoked in practical pipelines. For model evaluation, it additionally includes a built-in harness equipped with search orchestration and experience accumulation. Finally, we propose a new metric, the Speedup-Token Score (STS), which quantifies performance gains relative to token cost. By jointly considering speedup and cost, STS enables the assessment of practical kernel optimization capability of a model or harness under an acceptable budget. AscendGYM is available at https://github.com/SLAI-AITP/AscendGYM.

</details>


### [137] [Generated Evaluation Suites as Diversity-Driven Search for Real Agent Regressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17632)
*Pulkit Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过在$\tau^2$-bench代理中注入7个受控缺陷来分析评估套件的多样性驱动搜索方法，研究揭示了多样性驱动评估套件的关键特性，并建议评估档案应按单元格评分并公开覆盖率作为一级属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在评估基于需求、角色和场景类型的多样性驱动搜索算法生成的评估套件的质量，并探究可追溯缺陷如何影响评估结果。

Method: 通过在$\tau^2$-bench代理中跨两个领域注入7个受控缺陷，并评估这些缺陷对评估套件的影响

Result: 研究发现，对可追溯性的缺陷，天花板效应限制了其对评估套件的贡献，具体表现为4个可追溯的缺陷中有3个的效果低于检测阈值$\theta=0.10$。在一套固定的评估套件大小中，仅包含正面测试案例的评估套件无法检测到任何问题，多样化的评估套件在场景多样性方面能检测到更多的问题。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，评估套件应该根据单元格进行评分，并且应该公开覆盖率为第一级属性。同时，多样性的评估套件在特定情况下能够发现更多的问题。

Abstract: Generating an evaluation suite from an agent's specification is diversity-driven search: a generator fills a descriptor space (requirements $\times$ personas $\times$ scenario kinds), and the suite it emits is an archive, then scored like a benchmark by its mean pass rate, which throws away the search's product. We inject 7 controlled defects into a $τ^2$-bench agent across two domains. Coverage yields a ceiling: if a defect's traced footprint holds $b$ scenarios that pass at baseline, it can itself contribute at most $b/N$ to a suite of $N$. For 4 of 5 traceable defects that ceiling lies at or below the detection threshold $θ= 0.10$, so no within-footprint effect can move the suite past it; in both domains the tool-removal defect destroys every covered scenario it can (exactly its ceiling) while the suite-level pass rate moves the other way. Defects split into two regimes: trace-aligned ones, where per-cell scoring recovers the tool-removal defect in full and reveals a second as a significant improvement, and trace-orthogonal ones, whose footprint is behavioural: forced escalation is detected in retail at a traced coverage of exactly zero. Diversity along the generator's own axes helps only where it aligns: at fixed suite size a happy-path-only suite detects nothing, while a kind-diverse one detects the orchestration defect in 5 of 20 draws. Evaluation archives should be scored per cell and should publish coverage as a first-class property.

</details>


### [138] [What LLM Agents See and What Users Say](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17639)
*Yue Zhao,Angela Huyue Zhang,S. Alex Yang,Hua Wei,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了一个LLM代理在执行操作前验证信息的重要性，通过预设理论表明，在信息可能存在误差时，代理不应直接执行操作，而应让用户确认或提供更多信息。不同验证方式对代理行为的影响显著，列出所有结果可显著减少错误操作，而用户声明记录为空通常能有效避免代理误操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨了LLM代理在与用户交互过程中直接根据工具展示的信息或用户陈述执行操作的风险，引入了预设理论来指导代理何时应行动，以减少因信息不准确导致的错误行为。

Method: 通过对代理进行实验和在零售基准测试中添加特定场景，评估了不同验证方法（如列出记录、计数记录或让用户确认信息）对代理行为的控制效果。

Result: 结果显示，列出所有结果对于阻止代理根据误判信息行动效果显著，用户直接声明记录为空也能有效减少代理误操作的发生率。对于最新的代理模型，其处理此类情况的方式尤为谨慎，显示对潜在不确定性的规避能力强。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在设计LLM代理时，需要引入适当的机制确保代理在执行操作前进行充分的信息验证，提高系统的安全性与准确性。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent cancels a reservation or deletes a folder for a user, what stops it from acting on a false assumption: what its tools show, or what the user says? In this work, we study this gap with presupposition theory from linguistics: an agent may act only if each claim behind the act (e.g., one reservation fits the name) is seen to hold, answered by the user, or disclosed while the act can be undone. A simple bound shows that when lookups cannot tell one record from two, any agent acts without asking first as often with two records as with one. We then vary whether a search lists, counts, or omits a second match, and whether the user states a claim that a record refutes (e.g., "It is empty"). Under a role-only prompt, a count of a second record leaves most of 36 earlier agents acting on the one they see, whereas listing both stops most. When the user says a record such as a folder is empty, however, a count of its one item stops agents about as often as a listing, and stating the claim mostly cuts acting when it is false. The newest Claude and GPT models always open a counted second record, then never act. When we add a same-name, same-zip customer to a retail benchmark, a lookup returning that customer first led to wrong-account writes in about a quarter of conversations, and one that checks a street the user knows kept completion near the one-customer level. Safe writes therefore need lookups that list or check competing records, and evaluations that vary what tools show.

</details>


### [139] [Learn then Bet: Learning Interpretable Rules for Reliable Agent Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17662)
*You-Lin Chen,Xinyi Lisa Chen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal,Xiaojing Yu,Bingqing Song,Bin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeBet 是一个统计学习框架，通过学习一个可解释的 OR-of-AND 监控器，根据固定的投注统计量逐渐累积证据，并在达到预设阈值时介入。它在有限的内部类别可交换性和留一出稳定性的条件下提供了有误接受控制的有效性和检测能力的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的runtime监控器（如轮次限制和关键词过滤）可能会停止本该成功的运行或允许失败的运行继续。LeBet提出了一个新的统计学习框架来解决这个问题。

Method: LeBet通过学习可解释的OR-of-AND监控器，并利用固定的投注统计量来累积证据。它采用随机局部搜索来优化监控器的布尔结构，目的是提高经验上区分性，并在相同轨迹下进行学习和校准。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite上的实验中，LeBet的监控器节省了输入加输出的34.46%令牌，并使输出精度从18.33%提高到21.59%，经验误报率为13.64%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，LeBet能够有效地制定包含统计条件的可解释规则集的学习问题，并基于部分历史建立了训练条件下的可靠干预保证。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now run for hundreds of steps with little or no human supervision. In practice, practitioners use runtime monitors such as turn limits and keyword filters to decide when a run should be stopped, upgraded, or assisted. However, these heuristics may stop runs that would otherwise succeed or allow failing runs to continue. To address this limitation, we introduce LeBet (Learn then Bet), a statistical learning framework that derives its learning objective from sufficient conditions for valid and powerful intervention. In particular, LeBet learns an interpretable OR-of-AND monitor and accumulates evidence through a fixed betting statistic, intervening when the evidence reaches a prescribed threshold. Under finite within-class exchangeability and leave-one-out stability, we derive finite-sample guarantees for false-positive control (validity) and detection power conditional on the training data, using the same trajectories for learning and calibration without assuming independent and identically distributed trajectories. To address the intractable combinatorial optimization problem in our learning formulation, we use stochastic local search that leverages the monitor's Boolean structure to propose rule edits aimed at improving empirical separation. On SWE-bench Lite with Ministral 3 8B, the learned monitor saves $34.46\%$ of input-plus-output tokens and increases output precision from $18.33\%$ to $21.59\%$. The empirical false-positive rate is $13.64\%$. Our results show how to formalize the learning problem over interpretable rule sets with statistical conditions and establish training-conditional guarantees for reliable intervention based on partial histories.

</details>


### [140] [Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified? A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17680)
*Xintong Wang,Haihan Nan,Ruidong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了首个基于文本-历史关系的AIoT诊断基准，包括1762个文本窗口对，涵盖天气、维基百科和金融数据。研究评估了十种预训练模型在零样本学习下的文本选择和组合、以及误差诊断和基于时间尺度的修订控制的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于AIoT系统中时间序列数据与外部文本资料结合的需求日益增加，但当前对文本与历史数据一致或冲突时模型表现的认知仍不清楚，因此提出了这个新的基准来帮助理解不同文本类型如何影响模型预测。

Method: 研究收集了涉及天气、维基百科和金融市场的1762对文本-历史数据，并对十种不同预训练模型进行了零样本学习实验，探讨了不同文本类型的选择及结合策略，同时进行了误差分解和无训练修订控制规则的设计。

Result: 研究发现，在历史趋势一致的情境下，直接预报温度变化的文本比描述相关天气条件的文本更能减少模型误差；但在冲突情况下，仅使用预报类型的文本模型误差显著增加。通过结合两种文本类型，模型的均方归一化误差(MASE)降低了六个模型，准确性(准确性提高)提高了七个模型。无训练修订控制规则在四个存在误差增加问题的模型中表现最佳，降低了4.49%-21.98%的MASE并提高了2.99%-9.71%的方向准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究结果表明，理解文本数据如何增加或减少模型性能是AIoT领域的重要考虑因素，并提出了改进预测性能的方法。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems increasingly combine sensed time series with external text such as expert reports and news, yet how reliably models use text that agrees or conflicts with the sensed history remains unclear. We introduce the first real-world diagnostic benchmark organized by text-history relationship: 1,762 text-window pairs across sensor telemetry (Weather), web-service traffic (Wikipedia), and market data streams (Finance), each labeled Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified by whether the text's direction agrees with the historical trend, opposes it, or is not stated. Across ten pretrained models evaluated zero-shot, we investigate text selection and combination under text-history relationships (RQ1) and error diagnosis and horizon-scaled revision control (RQ2). For RQ1, we compare two types of text on paired Weather windows: one directly forecasts temperature changes, while the other describes related weather conditions, such as winds and fronts. Under Aligned, the first type yields the lowest error for most models. Under Conflicting, it increases most models' error above the no-text baseline; we therefore add only its stated magnitude and timing to the second type. Compared with either type alone, this combination lowers mean absolute scaled error (MASE) for six models and raises directional accuracy (DA) for seven. For RQ2, an error decomposition attributes the error increase to oversized revisions, and a training-free rule that limits them outperforms forecasts both with and without text for the four models with this trade-off, lowering MASE by 4.49%-21.98% and raising DA by 2.99%-9.71%. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/Shelton1202/ACU-Bench.

</details>


### [141] [OCTAVE: A Capability-Resolved Benchmark for End-to-End HTML5 Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17681)
*Kehang Li,Xinlan Xu,Yulin Jin,Heng Cao,Xuchen Li,Houxian Ren,Zihao Ji,Qiang Luo,Yadong Mao,Qilin He,Jian Zhang,Jian Zhao,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OCTAVE 提供了一个全面的游戏生成基准，包括8个能力维度和180项任务，评估生成游戏的完整性和丰富性，结果显示现有系统的性能在不同维度上差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前单一的综合评分机制无法准确反映游戏生成系统中的具体能力实现情况，OCTAVE 的引入旨在提供一个更加全面和细致的评估框架。

Method: OCTAVE 设计了一个基于HTML5的基准集，包含180项具体任务和1800项评估需求，涵盖PC和移动网页、2D和3D游戏。通过静态源代码判断来实现对各个能力维度和任务条件的诊断。

Result: 在11种模型的评估中，完成度和丰富度分别达到33.2%和32.5%，部分生成记录因为静态门控失败或缺失资源而失效。最好的组合仍然无法在公开和隐藏需求上达到50%的表现。

Conclusion: OCTAVE 拓展了评估范围，将其细化到了单一的评估需求、能力维度和任务条件级别，发现自动评估与人工判断之间存在显著分歧，主要集中在那些自认为满足但实际上失败的要求上。

Abstract: Complete game generation requires agents to coordinate rules, interactions, states, and presentation over a long development process. A single aggregate score obscures which capabilities are implemented and which requirements remain unmet. We present OCTAVE, a benchmark for complete HTML5 game generation organized around eight Capability Dimensions, with 180 tasks and 1,800 Evaluation Requirements drawn from real player requests, commercial-mechanics reconstruction, and human expert design. It covers PC Web, Mobile Web, 2D, and 3D. Requirements distinguish Completeness, assessing publicly specified core implementation, from Richness, assessing hidden, deeper system relationships. Static source-code judgments provide requirement-level diagnosis across capabilities and task conditions. Evaluation of 11 model-harness combinations yields 33.2% Completeness and 32.5% Richness; 19.9% of generation records are static gate failures or missing artifacts. Excluding same-model self-evaluation, even the best combination remains below 50% on both public and hidden requirements. Richness is not harder than Completeness under every task condition. Systems perform relatively better on Core Rules and Numerical Design, while AI/NPC and Feedback and Game Feel show more prominent gaps. Requirement difficulty offers coarse stratification but is only weakly negatively correlated with pass rates. A limited human cross-check finds that requirements judged satisfied by humans but failed by the automated evaluator are the main direction of disagreement. OCTAVE extends evaluation beyond aggregate scores to diagnosis localized by requirement, capability, and task condition.

</details>


### [142] [Co-Sight Harness: Externalized Control Assets and Deterministic Enforcement for Long-Horizon Business Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17696)
*Luyao Wang,Xijun Zhu,Zhaohui Hao,Xiwen Zhang,Xingyu Wen,Weishan Li,Yuyang Liao,Yanqin Gao,Yongsheng Du,Lingjun Huang,Shaowei Zhao,Gang Li,Qifan Yu,Baoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该方法通过将工作流定义、状态和领域知识移至版本控制资产，使用状态机和机器检查的接受闸门在执行过程中进行验证，并通过回归检查和人工批准更新资产。这种方法应用于三个超过200步的工作流，在一个领域中实现了全自动化完成率的提升，并成功由新的团队接管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于长时间业务流程中错误积累和用户请求与活动所需知识之间的词汇差异，研究是否能在不修改语言模型的情况下提高其可靠性。

Method: 保持核心部分不变，将工作流定义、状态和领域知识移至版本控制资产，使用状态机和机器检查的接受闸门在执行过程进行验证，通过回归检查和人工批准更新资产。

Result: 该方法在三个超过200步的工作流上进行了评估。在Domain A领域，全自动化完成率从50%提升到100%，检索召回率从56%提高到91.67%，端到端延迟从440分钟减少到159分钟，情景分析召回率从45%提升到93%。此外，其他两个领域也成功地由未编写资产的团队接管。

Conclusion: 该方法成功提高了长时间业务流程的可靠性，且能够在无需更改语言模型的情况下，通过严格的版本控制和执行验证机制确保流程的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon business workflows fail in two ways: errors accumulate as a chain of activities lengthens, and the knowledge an activity needs is written in a different vocabulary from the user's request. We study whether such workflows can be made reliable without changing the language model. Our method keeps the backbone fixed and moves the workflow definition, state and domain knowledge into versioned control assets outside the model. A state machine and machine-checkable acceptance gates enforce these assets during execution; an independent auditor grades the resulting trace. The assets are updated only after a regression check and human approval .We evaluate the method on three deployed workflows exceeding 200 steps. On Domain A, a 120-case end-to-end evaluation set shows unattended completion rising from 50% to 100%, retrieval recall from 56% to 91.67%, and end-to-end latency dropping from 440 to 159 minutes, with scenario-analysis recall rising from 45% to 93%. Two other domains were taken over by teams that did not write the assets, one in four days and one in seven days. A controlled ablation separates the two failure sources. The code is available at https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/master/Co-Sight%20Harness.

</details>


### [143] [An AI-enabled high-resolution chlorophyll-a forecasting model that outperforms the operational global biogeochemical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17704)
*Yong Jiang,Zheng Jiang,Gaowei Zhang,Zengzhou Hao,Suixiang Shi,Chi Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChlCast 是一种使用深度学习的 AI 模型，能够提供高分辨率的表面叶绿素-a 浓度预测，相较于传统模型，其预测精度显著提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模型在耦合物理传输和生物过程模拟时存在计算需求高、预测精度有限的问题。ChlCast 通过结合元素化时空卷积和多尺度架构，提高模型在预测表面叶绿素-a 浓度时的精度。

Method: ChlCast 使用深度学习方法，通过元素化时空卷积和多尺度架构结合历史叶绿素-a 场景和气候信息进行学习。

Result: ChlCast 在与 Copernicus 全球海洋生物地球化学预报系统的对比中，其均方误差和绝对误差分别减少了 47.63% 和 70.74%。与独立的 BGC-Argo 现场观测数据对比时，误差分别减少了 13.07% 和 35.03%。高分辨率（约 4km）的数据展示出了海岸线等地形特征。

Conclusion: ChlCast 的高精度和高分辨率预测表明了基于观测的深度学习在高分辨率表面叶绿素-a 可变性预报中的潜力。

Abstract: Surface chlorophyll-a concentration is a key indicator of marine phytoplankton variability, and short-term forecasts of its distribution provide advance information for marine ecosystem monitoring and management. However, interactions between physical transport and biological processes complicate chlorophyll-a forecasting, and simulating these coupled processes with conventional numerical models imposes substantial computational demands. Here we introduce ChlCast, an AI model that generates daily forecasts of surface chlorophyll-a concentration over a 10-day horizon at approximately 4-km resolution between 60$^\circ$S and 60$^\circ$N. ChlCast combines factorized spatiotemporal convolutions with a multiscale architecture to learn from historical chlorophyll-a fields and climatological information. Compared with the operational 0.25$^\circ$ Copernicus global ocean biogeochemical forecasting system, ChlCast reduces mean absolute error and root mean square error by 70.74% and 47.63%, respectively, against satellite-derived Level-4 chlorophyll-a fields, and by 35.03% and 13.07% against independent BGC-Argo in situ observations.
